# Finance and Trading

[All categories](../README.md#categories) · 1,090 connectors · Last updated November 23, 2026

Connectors marked **`A`** are built and maintained by Anthropic; **`C`** marks the in-app catalog's Community badge (third-party built, not vetted like web-directory entries). Methodology and the held-entry policy are in the [README](../README.md) and [CONTRIBUTING.md](../CONTRIBUTING.md).

## Accounting Payroll and Financial Planning

- [0Delta.ai](https://www.0delta.ai) **`C`** - Queries Exact Online accounting data and curated finance reports for cash runway, burn, P&L, open invoices, and forecast-versus-actuals across one company or a portfolio. *Use case: Spotting which portfolio company is burning cash fastest, pulling open invoices before a board call, comparing this quarter's forecast against posted actuals.*
- [aarven](https://aarven.ch) **`C`** - Reads balance sheets, income statements, accounts and bookings in Swiss Aarven books, prepares Swiss VAT returns, and drafts bookings from descriptions or invoices for posting after confirmation. *Use case: Checking bank balances and receivables as of quarter end, preparing a net tax rate VAT return, drafting a booking from a supplier invoice.*
- [AawazAI](https://aawazai.com) **`C`** - Lists a chartered accountant practice's GST filing workspaces and their status in AawazAI, shows a workspace's stage, financials and open issues, and summarizes a client's books for a period with GSTR-2B reconciliation ties. *Use case: Checking which client GST workspaces are stuck before the filing deadline, reviewing a client's October books summary and unreconciled purchases.*
- [Abacum.ai](https://www.abacum.ai) **`C`** - Get live actuals, plans and forecasts from Abacum without leaving Claude. *Use case: Checking this quarter's actuals against the forecast before a board meeting, pulling next month's plan without opening a separate tool.*
- [AccLyf Books](https://acclyfbooks.com) **`C`** - Reads AccLyf Books accounting data, including profit and loss, balance sheet, cash flow, invoices, bills, bank balances, receivables and payables aging, sales by customer or product and expenses by supplier. *Use case: Checking which customers have invoices more than 60 days overdue, comparing this quarter's profit with last quarter's.*
- [accountai](https://accountai.ai) **`C`** - Returns trial balance, profit and loss, balance sheet, cash, VAT, aged receivables and payables, stock, fixed asset, loan and payroll reports from AccountAI books under UAE, Bahrain, Qatar, Oman, OHADA or standard frameworks, and creates and posts invoices, bills, credit notes, journals, payments, stock adjustments and assets. *Use case: Pulling receivables more than 60 days overdue, creating and posting an invoice for a new customer, running this month's depreciation.*
- [AdminPulse](https://www.adminpulse.eu) **`C`** - Reads clients, contacts, companies, team members, documents, invoices, time registrations and compliance data in an AdminPulse accounting practice, and creates and updates relations, to-dos, interactions and time registrations, adds documents and sends emails, asking for approval before anything that changes AdminPulse data. *Use case: Listing clients with missing compliance data, logging two hours of bookkeeping for a client, creating a to-do to chase missing bank statements.*
- [AFAS AI Connect, by iWebDevelopment](https://www.iwebdevelopment.com/en/afas/ai-mcp-connector) **`C`** - Queries AFAS Profit invoices, revenue, stock, quotes, orders, work orders, cash flow and aging, and prepares journal entries, invoices, purchase orders, reorder levels, business relations and registered hours as drafts that run only after explicit confirmation. *Use case: Looking up a record in a connected administration system, updating an entry without switching applications.*
- [Aiwyn Tax (formerly Column Tax)](https://aiwyn.ai) - Estimates federal and state taxes with Aiwyn's tax engine from tax documents such as W-2s and returns a 1040 PDF. *Use case: Estimating federal and state tax from a W-2, producing a 1040 PDF to review.*
- [Aleph](https://getaleph.com) - FP&A platform joining ERP, CRM, HRIS, spreadsheet, and warehouse data into a governed source of truth for plain-language questions, reports, and analysis. *Use case: Building a headcount-versus-plan report for a board meeting, tracing a variance back to the ERP line behind it, comparing margin across two quarters.*
- [AluguelManager](https://aluguelmanager.com) **`C`** - Reads Brazilian landlords' lease contracts, rent receipts, IPTU instalments, alerts and dashboard summaries, calculates amounts owed with late penalties and interest, index-linked rent increases, proration and business-day due dates, records payments and manages contract and IPTU records. *Use case: Checking which tenants are late this month and what they owe with multa e juros, recording a rent payment, reviewing the IPTU instalments still due.*
- [Alvore Finance](https://www.alvore.com.br) **`C`** - Reads company and personal financial data on the Alvore Consultoria platform, including income statements, cash flow, budgets, categorized Open Finance transactions and ERP inventory, and classifies transactions, records stock movements and creates consulting projects and HTML artifacts. *Use case: Reviewing a client company's monthly income statement and cash flow, reclassifying miscategorized bank transactions, checking a household's monthly budget against actual spending.*
- [Amadeo](https://amadeo.ge) **`C`** - Reads Amadeo financial data for Georgian businesses, including profit and loss, cash flow, receivables and payables aging, inventory, KPIs, budgets versus actuals and revenue and expenses by branch, client and group. *Use case: Comparing Q3 revenue by branch with Q2, listing receivables more than 60 days overdue, checking budget against actuals for marketing.*
- [Ambrook](https://ambrook.com) **`C`** - Reads Ambrook transactions, invoices, bills, estimates, accounts, contacts, attachments, profit and loss and balance sheets for farms and agricultural businesses, and searches help articles. *Use case: Listing unpaid invoices for a cattle ranch, pulling the profit and loss for the 2025 harvest season, finding bills due this week.*
- [Anrok](https://anrok.com) - Reads Anrok sales tax and VAT data, including transactions, filings, nexus status, customers, exemption certificates and product mappings, estimates tax, and creates transactions, customers, certificates and products. *Use case: Checking nexus status in Texas after a strong quarter, estimating tax on a 12,000-dollar invoice to a customer in Ontario, uploading a resale certificate.*
- [Antragsradar](https://antragsradar.de) **`C`** - Reads a consulting team's Antragsradar table of German R&D tax credit applications synced from the BSFZ portal, with companies, documents, follow-up requests and history. *Use case: Listing applications with open BSFZ follow-up requests this week, reading the latest document for one client.*
- [Aprilis](https://aprilisgo.com) **`C`** - Uploads contracts, order forms, amendments and schedules to Aprilis and builds technical accounting workpapers and memos, such as ASC 606 revenue recognition and ASC 805 purchase accounting, with build status and download links. *Use case: Building an ASC 606 memo for a three-year SaaS contract with an implementation fee, drafting purchase accounting for a small acquisition.*
- [Arbrain MCP](https://www.arbrain.com.br) **`C`** - Reads ArBrain ERP income statements by period with drill-down to entries, the chart of accounts and budgets against actuals, with rankings of categories and deviations, read-only. *Use case: Comparing this quarter's income statement with last year's for a brewery, finding the expense categories most over budget in 2026.*
- [AuditFile](https://www.auditfile.com) **`C`** - Reads AuditFile audit, review and compilation engagements, including clients, staff, materiality, progress, the section and procedure tree with sign-off state, trial balances, accounts, adjustments, analytical reviews, findings, procedure notes, workpapers with download links and saved reports. *Use case: Checking which procedures in an engagement still lack reviewer sign-off, summarizing open findings for a client, pulling trial balance adjustments for a year-end review.*
- [Aurgus](https://aurgus.com) **`C`** - Computes rebate, chargeback, billback and deduction closes from uploaded POS, claims or credits extracts, tracing each figure to its source row, rule and agreement, exporting an auditor-ready deliverable and explaining accounting treatment of rebate types. *Use case: Computing a quarter's open rebate accrual from a claims file, tracing a chargeback figure to its agreement, checking the ASC 606 treatment of a volume rebate.*
- [Azuma](https://software.azuma.co/claude/) **`C`** - Looks up and updates an accounting firm's Azuma practice data, including clients, contacts, engagements, filing periods, tasks, comments, time entries, invoices and workpapers, and clocks time in and out. *Use case: Listing overdue tax return tasks for this week, logging two hours on a client engagement, marking an invoice paid.*
- [Balances](https://www.balances.ai) **`C`** - Runs small-business bookkeeping, categorizing and reconciling bank transactions, sending invoices to customers, logging bills and filing receipts, reviewing financial statements, generating reports including 1099-NEC reports, forecasting cash flow and tracking unpaid customer balances. *Use case: Reconciling last month's bank statement against the books, sending an overdue invoice to a client, forecasting cash flow for the next quarter.*
- [Basee](https://basee.io) **`C`** - Clocks in and out, records time away, requests leave, registers expenses with receipt files, approves, rejects or relays pending approvals, searches customers, estimates and invoices, and creates estimate and invoice drafts in a Japanese-language back-office suite for Japanese service businesses. *Use case: Clocking in at the start of a shift, filing a taxi receipt as an out-of-pocket expense, drafting an invoice for a customer's finished project.*
- [Beiing Human for Foundation](https://beiinghuman.com) **`C`** - Reads a Foundation Software construction accounting ERP, covering payables, receivables, retainage, job cost, WIP, change orders, commitments, GL activity, bank reconciliation and payroll, and, when allowed, stages a fixed set of writes such as timecards, purchase orders, journal entries, jobs, customers and invoices, each previewed and made only after a person approves it, with rollback. *Use case: Checking pay-when-paid exposure on a job, reviewing the WIP schedule, staging this week's timecards.*
- [Bilbee](https://bilbee.com.au) **`C`** - Reads an Australian sole trader's Bilbee invoices, quotes, clients, expenses, time, BAS quarter and tax estimate, and creates invoice and quote drafts, clients, time entries and expenses for review. *Use case: Checking the estimated tax still to pay this financial year, drafting an invoice for a plumbing job, logging three hours.*
- [Bilem](https://bilem.ai) **`C`** - Reads a 1C:Enterprise accounting database through Bilem, including cash balances, counterparties, receivables and payables, and prints invoices, powers of attorney and reconciliation statements as PDF, read-only. *Use case: Checking cash on hand, finding customers who owe more than 30 days, printing a reconciliation statement for a supplier.*
- [Bill (South Africa)](https://bill.co.za) **`C`** - Reads a South African law practice's Bill clients, matters, time entries, invoices, payments, trust transfers, statements and debtor ageing, and adds clients, matters, and draft time entries and expenses that wait for approval in Bill. *Use case: Listing clients who owe more than 90 days, pulling a matter statement, adding a draft time entry for a consultation.*
- [bimetrics](https://bimetrics.de) **`C`** - Searches receipts, bookings and bank transactions in bimetrics accounting, checks open items, matches payments, uploads receipts, and drafts and finalizes invoices, ZUGFeRD e-invoices and quotes to prepare month-end close before DATEV export. *Use case: Matching an incoming bank payment to an open invoice, uploading a stack of PDF receipts at month-end, issuing a ZUGFeRD e-invoice to a corporate client.*
- [Blond](https://blond.swiss) **`C`** - Reads a Blond account's customers, invoices, projects, time, bank transactions, balances and cash flow, and drafts quotes and invoices, bills projects and updates contacts, products and payment orders. *Use case: Listing overdue invoices, checking hours spent on a client project, turning an accepted quote into an invoice.*
- [bobalice](https://bobalice.com) **`C`** - Keeps double-entry books through bobalice, reading bank transactions, balances, documents and the ledger, and booking signed, chained entries from receipts and bank feeds. *Use case: Booking a client dinner receipt as entertainment, matching a bank transaction, checking the bank balance.*
- [booked.](https://bookedplatform.com) **`C`** - Works with an accounting firm's booked. client books, reading reports, ledgers and reconciliations, categorizing transactions, managing rules, vendors and journal entries, and queueing month-end work for approval. *Use case: Categorizing a client's uncoded card transactions, checking the bank reconciliation, queueing the October close.*
- [BooksGPT](https://booksgpt.ai) **`C`** - Runs BooksGPT cloud accounting, reading balances, profit and loss, cash flow, aging, invoices, bills, projects, job costs and inventory, and preparing bills, invoices, customers, vendors and other changes that post only after approval in BooksGPT. *Use case: Checking job cost against budget on a kitchen remodel, preparing a bill from a supplier receipt, reading receivables aging.*
- [Booksmate](https://booksmate.com) **`C`** - Fetches invoices and receipts from Gmail or Microsoft mailboxes, extracts data from uploaded documents, queries spend by category or vendor, finds duplicate payments, and exports documents to Xero or Google Drive. *Use case: Pulling last month's supplier receipts from a Gmail inbox, checking for a vendor invoice paid twice, exporting a quarter's documents to Xero for the accountant.*
- [Boongon](https://boongon.com) **`C`** - Reads one company's accounting records in read-only mode, returning overdue customer invoices, unpaid supplier bills, customer balances, recent invoices, a company summary and digital workforce activity. *Use case: Listing invoices more than 30 days overdue, checking which supplier bills fall due this week, looking up a customer's outstanding balance.*
- [Breezing](https://breezing.io) - Crypto accounting that imports 70+ blockchains and exchanges, computes cost basis and books journals to Xero, QuickBooks or Bexio, asking for confirmation before any write. *Use case: Reconciling a wallet's transactions for month end, computing realized gains for a tax filing, posting crypto journals to Xero.*
- [Briefcase](https://briefcase.so) **`C`** - Pulls profit and loss, balance sheet and aged debtor reports from Briefcase Ledger for UK businesses and their accountants, reviews coded transactions, publishes transactions, raises invoices, posts journals and runs month-end close items, including for Xero and QuickBooks books, asking before every change and previewing anything that posts to the books. *Use case: Pulling a client's aged debtors before a credit-control call, posting an accrual journal at month-end, raising a sales invoice for a finished project.*
- [Budgetic](https://budgetic.eu) **`C`** - Reads income statements, cash plans and balance sheets in Budgetic, a French financial planning tool for SMEs and associations, by month, fiscal year, scenario, entity or analytic family, compares budget with actuals, traces cells to ledger entries, calculates ratios, produces tables and charts, and proposes, applies and reverts change plans. *Use case: Ranking the drivers of a budget-versus-actual gap in marketing spend, tracing a surprising March figure back to ledger entries, preparing a corrective entry for review.*
- [Bulk Receipt Scanner](https://bulkreceiptscanner.com) **`C`** - Searches, checks and corrects receipts scanned in bulk with Bulk Receipt Scanner, flags duplicates, assigns jobs, exports CSV and sends receipts to QuickBooks Online after asking first. *Use case: Finding all receipts from one vendor in October, fixing a misread total, sending a client's receipts to QuickBooks.*
- [Business Agent](https://business.appimate.com) **`C`** - Reviews and manages an Appimate Business Agent workspace, including business snapshots, projects and tasks, invoices, customers, products, assets, and annual financial statements with ledger entries, notes and trial balances. *Use case: Getting this month's business snapshot, listing unpaid invoices, adding a ledger entry to the 2026 annual financial statements.*
- [Calcu.mx — Calculadoras de México](https://calcu.mx) **`C`** - Calculates Mexican net salary with ISR and IMSS contributions plus employer cost, adds or removes 16% or 8% border IVA, updates amounts for inflation with INEGI's INPC since 1990, estimates IMSS Ley 73 pensions and computes liquidación and finiquito severance, each with a breakdown and the tables' effective date. *Use case: Working out take-home pay on a 25,000-peso monthly gross salary, estimating the severance owed after an unjustified dismissal, adjusting a 2015 rent amount to the latest INPC month.*
- [Calim](https://calim.com.ar) **`C`** - Issues and cancels sales invoices with CAE in ARCA, records purchase invoices, reads and approves VAT and gross income tax settlements, checks tax debts and monotributo status, and loads payroll changes in a Calim accounting account. *Use case: Invoicing a client 250,000 pesos for October consulting, checking how much VAT is due this month, loading overtime for two employees into payroll.*
- [Campfire](https://campfire.ai) - Reads Campfire ERP accounts, transactions, vendors, contracts, budgets, departments, entities, tags and aging, and pulls balance sheets, income statements and cash flow statements. *Use case: Pulling financial statements for a board deck, checking aging and vendor transactions.*
- [cash-service.pl](https://cash-service.pl) **`C`** - Calculates Polish net salary, civil-contract pay, B2B versus employment, ZUS and health contributions, loan instalments and tax late-payment interest, and looks up companies on the VAT whitelist by NIP, validates bank account numbers, and finds the competent tax office, court or bailiff, postal codes, NBP exchange rates and ZUS/PIT indicators. *Use case: Comparing take-home pay on a B2B contract versus employment, checking a supplier's VAT whitelist status and bank accounts by NIP, finding the tax office for a Kraków address.*
- [Cashflowy](https://cashflowy.ai) **`C`** - Provides read-only access to Cashflowy bookkeeping, including transactions, revenue and expenses, invoices, clients, accounts, uncategorized transactions, tax estimates, safe owner pay and report exports. *Use case: Checking how much can be paid out to the owner this month, listing uncategorized transactions before closing the books.*
- [Check](https://checkhq.com) - Manages companies, employees, contractors, payrolls, tax parameters, filings, payments, benefits and embedded components through the Check Payroll API, and runs and approves payrolls and generates reports. *Use case: Onboarding a new restaurant client with four employees, running and approving its biweekly payroll, generating a census report.*
- [CherryData](https://cherrymoney.co.uk) **`C`** - Reads ledger-calculated financial reports from a Cherry Money organisation, returning profit and loss for explicit dates, comparisons of two periods, trial balances and balance sheets, without creating or editing any records. *Use case: Comparing this quarter's profit and loss with the same quarter last year, pulling the trial balance at month end, checking the balance sheet before a board meeting.*

- [COMMITLY mcp-reviewer](https://commitly.com) **`C`** - Reads bank balances, accounts, credit lines, transactions, invoices and liquidity forecasts with daily balance curves for plans and scenarios, runs a health check covering cash burn, runway and a health score, creates and updates invoices, creates and recategorizes transactions, creates, updates and deletes forecast, plan and scenario budgets, and adds comments with mentions. *Use case: Spotting the tightest week in next quarter's cash forecast, building a scenario budget for a delayed customer payment, recategorizing last month's uncategorized transactions.*

- [CONA](https://getcona.com) **`C`** - Reviews accounting configuration, integrations, VAT settings, reconciliation status, posting routes, and anomalies, and previews reconciliation proposals. *Use case: Checking VAT settings for a new sales channel, investigating a reconciliation anomaly, previewing proposed matches before posting.*

- [Countz Accounting](https://www.countz.ai) **`C`** - Supplies accounting analysis playbooks, such as ledger tie-outs, cash and payables reconciliations, quality-of-earnings bridges and revenue and lease accounting tests, to the Countz Accounting plugin. *Use case: Loading the playbook for reconciling payables to supplier statements, retrieving the steps for a quality-of-earnings EBITDA bridge, pulling the ASC 842 lease accounting test playbook.*

- [DebtStack](https://debtstack.tools) **`C`** - Models debt facilities for lending businesses from origination plans, loan terms and facility assumptions, showing monthly cash flows, funding needs and equity requirements, compares changes to advance rates, borrowing costs, growth or credit losses, and saves named scenarios. *Use case: Estimating how much equity a consumer lending startup needs in its first year, testing an advance rate cut from 85 to 75 percent, saving a named scenario for a board discussion.*

- [Switchbooks](https://switchbooks.ai) **`C`** - Reads profit and loss, balance sheet, cash flow, trial balance, forecasts and ledgers, and with permission categorizes, splits and posts bank transactions, books journal entries, manages payees and rules, and finishes reconciliations across client companies. *Use case: Categorizing last month's unreviewed card transactions, booking a depreciation journal entry at quarter end, finishing the March bank reconciliation for one of several client companies.*

- [TapTax](https://taptax.co.uk) **`C`** - Shows a UK sole trader's or landlord's tax position and Making Tax Digital deadlines, records and recategorizes income and expenses, logs mileage, creates, sends and marks invoices paid, and prepares quarterly updates for filing in TapTax. *Use case: Checking how much tax is owed so far this year, logging a receipt for a van repair, preparing the quarterly update before the next deadline.*
- [Tax Hero AI](https://taxhero.vc) **`C`** - Reads Tax Hero businesses, tax deadlines and statuses, document inventory, subscription and processing status. *Use case: Checking upcoming tax deadlines for a business, seeing which documents are still missing.*
- [Tax1099Payer](https://www.tax1099.com) **`C`** - Creates and updates payers in Tax1099 without sending TINs and checks pending payer status. *Use case: Adding a new payer company before filing 1099-NEC forms, checking whether a payer is still pending.*
- [Taxapp.nz](https://taxapp.nz) **`C`** - Reads a New Zealand sole trader's or landlord's tax year overview, tax summary, profit and loss, rental report, income and expense records, contacts, properties and bank reconciliation status, and adds income, expense and contact records when permitted. *Use case: Checking indicative tax for the current year, pulling the rental report for a Wellington flat, adding a plumber's invoice as an expense.*
- [TaxDome](https://taxdome.com) **`C`** - Manages tax, accounting and bookkeeping firm work, finding and updating clients, contacts, jobs, tasks, notes, documents and tax returns, reading bills, payments and proposals, sending chat and SMS messages, and lets a firm's clients see outstanding requests, return status and balances, fill in questionnaires and reply to the firm. *Use case: Listing overdue jobs in the tax-season pipeline, getting a brief on a client before a call, messaging a client about a missing document.*
- [Taxley Company Tax Tools](https://taxley.co.uk) **`C`** - Estimates UK Corporation Tax and runs calculators for filing and payment deadlines, late filing penalties and interest, section 455 tax on director's loans, capital allowances, chargeable gains with indexation, company size and long accounting periods. *Use case: Estimating Corporation Tax on £180,000 of profit, checking the payment deadline for a March year end, working out the section 455 charge on an overdrawn director's loan.*
- [TDE](https://tdehq.com) **`C`** - Prices import and export deals month by month with TDE, including cash runway, landed cost, duty, quote and lane comparisons, cost scenarios, margin cushion and risk register. *Use case: Checking whether a 40-container order from Malaysia into the UK breaks the cash floor, comparing two freight quotes.*
- [Tegement](https://tegement.com) **`C`** - Queries an Argentine small-business management system's customers, products, stock, invoices, purchases, collections, cheques and cash accounts with database-computed totals, downloads built-in reports such as the VAT ledger as CSV, creates and updates customers, invoices, receipts, delivery notes, products and stock adjustments, and deletes records, asking for confirmation before creating, editing or deleting anything. *Use case: Totaling last month's sales to a customer, downloading the VAT ledger for the accountant, issuing an invoice and delivery note for a new order.*
- [Temlavo](https://temlavo.com) **`C`** - Extracts vendor, customer, invoice number, dates, currency, line items, tax and totals from invoice PDFs or images submitted by HTTPS link into structured JSON, runs totals, tax, line-item, currency and duplicate checks with a completed, needs-review or failed outcome, and reports processed-page usage. *Use case: Extracting line items from a supplier invoice PDF, checking whether an invoice is a possible duplicate, reviewing which invoices failed the tax arithmetic check.*
- [ThinkOut](https://thinkout.io) **`C`** - Reads ThinkOut workspaces, balances, transactions, transfers, counterparties, categories, labels and transaction rules, and summarizes cash flow, budgets, forecasts and scenarios. *Use case: Summarizing recent cash flow, reviewing a forecast or a scenario, checking balances against budgets.*
- [Tilit](https://tilit.ai) **`C`** - Reads Tilit bookkeeping data, including financial reports, ledger entries, sales and purchase invoices, receipts, bank balances, VAT returns, payroll summaries, open invoice aging, project profitability and letters from the Finnish Tax Administration. *Use case: Checking which sales invoices are more than 30 days overdue, summarizing a letter from the Finnish Tax Administration, comparing project profitability this fiscal year.*
- [TinyCashflow](https://tinycashflow.com) **`C`** - Forecasts account balances on any date from TinyCashflow accounts, transactions and recurring items, reports spending by category, records and edits transactions and recurring items, and undoes the last change, asking first for balance updates, large amounts and bigger changes, and requires TinyCashflow Premium. *Use case: Checking whether the checking account dips below zero before the 28th, logging a 120-dollar electricity bill, pausing a gym membership from next month.*
- [tofu AI Bookkeeping](https://www.gotofu.com) **`C`** - Uploads bills, receipts, sales invoices, credit notes and bank statements for AI extraction, finds and corrects extracted accounts, tax codes, totals and line items, manages knowledge-card rules, publishes approved documents to accounting software such as Xero, QuickBooks, freee, Sage, Zoho and MYOB, and maintains entities, charts of accounts, tax rates, contacts, members and integrations. *Use case: Uploading a batch of supplier receipts and fixing tax codes the extraction got wrong, publishing approved bills to Xero, adding a rule that maps a utility vendor to one account.*
- [Tonbab](https://www.tonbab.com) **`C`** - Reads Tonbab ERP data for Thai businesses, including deals, quotations, invoices, AR and AP aging, stock levels, purchase orders, work orders, service jobs, employees, leave and profit and loss across companies. *Use case: Listing customers more than 60 days overdue, checking stock below reorder point in the main warehouse, summarizing this month's sales for two companies.*
- [tripfix Reisekosten](https://www.tripfix.de) **`C`** - Lists and reads business trips and expense reports in tripfix, creates trips as drafts, and calculates German mileage and per diem allowances using official BMF rates for all listed countries. *Use case: Creating a draft trip with mileage to a client in Munich, calculating per diems for a three-day trip to France, listing this year's trips.*
- [Twinfield AI Connect, by iWebDevelopment](https://www.iwebdevelopment.com/en/twinfield/ai-mcp-connector) **`C`** - Queries Twinfield invoices, aging, ledger transactions, VAT positions, bookings per journal or period and cash flow, and prepares journal entries, payment reminders and debtor and creditor updates as drafts that run only after explicit confirmation. *Use case: Checking a client's outstanding invoice, reviewing last month's ledger transactions, asking for a summary of bookkeeping entries from a specific week.*
- [V+ Accounting](https://www.vplusaccounting.com) **`C`** - Reads V+ Accounting companies, income statements, balance sheets, trial balances, receivables and payables aging and bank account reconciliation status. *Use case: Pulling a holding company's Q3 balance sheet, listing receivables more than 60 days overdue.*
- [wals.pro AI 4 weclapp](https://wals.pro) **`C`** - Searches weclapp ERP records, reports on revenue, open items, orders and stock, reviews replenishment proposals, and prepares previewed changes to customers, quotations, orders, purchase orders, articles and tickets that run only after explicit confirmation. *Use case: Checking which customers have overdue open items this month, reviewing replenishment proposals for low-stock articles, drafting a purchase order for a supplier and confirming it after preview.*
- [Wazen ERP](https://erp.wazen.sa) **`C`** - Runs sales, purchase, inventory, items and account statement reports in Wazen, a Saudi cloud ERP, reads Task Manager projects and tasks, creates projects, and creates, updates, moves and deletes tasks, previewing every write and applying it only after approval. *Use case: Pulling last quarter's sales report by branch, checking stock levels in a store, assigning a follow-up task to a teammate.*
- [Well](https://wellapp.ai) **`C`** - Query and act on your entire business from Claude: invoices, contacts, transactions, accounts, and the 140+ tools you already use, unified in one context graph. *Use case: Sending an invoice through a connected tool, checking a contact's transaction history across systems, updating an account without switching between 140+ apps.*
- [Wisdom Accounts](https://marothiatechs.com/support/wisdom-accounts-connector) **`C`** - Reads Wisdom Accounts GST books, including outstanding balances, sales and purchases, bills, ledger statements, party details and profit and loss, and prepares bills, payments and expense entries, saving them only after confirmation. *Use case: Checking which parties owe more than 50,000 rupees, recording a cash payment to a fabric supplier, pulling September's sales summary.*
- [World Office Cloud](https://www.worldoffice.com.co) **`C`** - Reads World Office accounting reports from questions in Spanish, covering sales by product, salesperson and document, purchases by product, supplier and document, balance sheet, income statement, auxiliary ledger, account statements, warehouse inventory and payroll, read-only. *Use case: Consultando el saldo de una cuenta antes de pagar a un proveedor, revisando los movimientos del mes pasado, preguntando por el balance general de la empresa.*
- [Wrapbook](https://www.wrapbook.com) **`C`** - Reads production payroll and accounting data across shows, including payrolls, budgets, chart of accounts lines, journal entries, purchase orders, AP invoices, vendors, vendor payments and transaction lines, and runs reports. *Use case: Finding every AP invoice from a lighting vendor, pulling journal entries for a single show, running a budget report for a production.*
- [X.Tax MCP](https://x.tax) **`C`** - Lists business tax filings for Forms 1065, 1120-S, 1120, 7004 and 4868, checks IRS acceptance status, lists supporting documents and downloads filed return PDFs from an X.Tax account. *Use case: Checking whether an S-corp return was accepted by the IRS, listing the K-1s attached to a partnership filing, downloading a filed return PDF.*
- [Xero](https://www.xero.com) - Reads Xero financials with read-only access, covering profit and loss, financial and cash position, invoices, bills, aged receivables and payables, top customers, purchase orders, bank transactions, chart of accounts and 1099 summaries, with year-over-year comparisons and links back to Xero. *Use case: Reviewing aged receivables before chasing late invoices, comparing this year's profit and loss with last year's.*
- [Xflow](https://www.xflowpay.com) **`C`** - Shows live currency conversion rates, FX forecasts and historical rate charts as interactive widgets, including USD to INR conversion. *Use case: Checking a live USD to INR rate before a transfer, viewing a currency's historical rate chart, pulling an FX forecast.*
- [xpnAi](https://xpnai.co) **`C`** - Analyzes general ledger, payables and receivables across multiple Xero organisations, prepares accruals, reversing journals, prepaid expense and deferred revenue spreads and intercompany invoice pairs as drafts after the full proposal is reviewed, and, with explicit write permission, aligns charts of accounts and tracking categories directly. *Use case: Comparing intercompany balances across three entities at month-end, spreading a prepaid insurance expense over twelve months, drafting a matched intercompany invoice and bill.*
- [XpressBI](https://bi.technosoftcr.com) **`C`** - Reads XpressBI financial statements from Contador Xpress and sales summaries and product sales from Facel for the user's companies. *Use case: Comparing this month's revenue with last year for a Costa Rican company, listing top-selling products.*
- [Zen365](https://zen365.ai) **`C`** - Reads Zen365 books for businesses in Kosovo, including sales, purchases, entity balances, bank transactions, reports and VAT, and creates sales and entities. *Use case: Checking which customers owe more than 1,000 euro, recording a new sale to a Prishtina client.*
- [Zoho Books](https://www.zoho.com/books) - Online accounting and finance operations. *Use case: Invoicing, expense management, bank reconciliation, financial reporting within the Zoho ecosystem.*
- [マネーフォワード クラウド会計](https://biz.moneyforward.com/accounting) **`C`** - Reads マネーフォワード クラウド会計 office details, accounts, departments, journals, transactions and trade partners, pulls trial balance and transition reports for the balance sheet and profit and loss, creates and updates journals and journal rules, and creates transactions and trade partners. *Use case: 先月の特定の取引先への仕訳を確認する、試算表を比較する、四半期末に貸借対照表を自然言語で確認する。*

## Business Banking and Spend Management

- [AllClear Lending](https://allclearcapital.com) **`C`** - Finds named US business and investor lenders by industry, state and product, ranks the cheapest suitable programs for a borrower profile, explains state brokering rules and suggests the next option after a decline. *Use case: Finding SBA lenders that fund a dental practice in Ohio, checking broker licensing rules in California, picking a fallback after a bank declines a trucking loan.*
- [Allowance](https://useallowance.com) **`C`** - Issues scoped one-time virtual cards for agent purchases after a human approves a spending allowance, and reports purchase results, receipts, and fulfillment status. *Use case: Requesting a $200 approval before ordering a replacement part, issuing a single-use card at checkout, checking whether a shipment was fulfilled after payment cleared.*
- [Altitude](https://support.altitude.xyz) **`C`** - Lists Altitude business accounts, workspaces, members, cards and tasks, searches transactions, finds bill and receipt forwarding addresses, and generates and downloads statements and exports. *Use case: Searching last month's card transactions over 500 dollars, finding the receipt forwarding address, downloading the September statement.*
- [AppVentory](https://www.appventory.com) **`C`** - Answers read-only questions about a tracked software portfolio, including which apps are in use, where SaaS spend goes and the top saving opportunities, for a single business or across a tech advisor's client book. *Use case: Listing the largest subscription costs in a company's SaaS stack, spotting apps nobody uses ahead of a budget review, comparing saving opportunities across several clients.*
- [Atlar](https://www.atlar.com) - Queries live treasury data across bank accounts, balances, cash flows, transactions, payments, forecasts, and treasury policies under existing role permissions. *Use case: Checking consolidated cash before a payment run, reconciling a transaction against a bank statement, reviewing a forecast ahead of a board meeting.*
- [AuraVMS](https://www.auravms.com) **`C`** - Searches and adds suppliers, drafts and sends RFQs with line items, compares returned quotes with L1/L2/L3 price ranking, reminds non-responders, places purchase orders and closes RFQs, asking before sending RFQs, reminders or purchase orders and before closing an RFQ. *Use case: Sending an RFQ for 500 steel brackets to three suppliers, comparing the quotes that came back, placing a purchase order with the lowest bidder.*
- [Australian Business Loan Finder](https://www.splendidfi.com/tools/business-loan-finder) **`C`** - Shortlists Australian bank, non-bank and private business lenders whose published loan amount, time trading, revenue and security criteria fit a stated need, across business loans, lines of credit, invoice, equipment, vehicle, property, franchise and SMSF finance, with lender profiles and finance-type explanations but no interest rates. *Use case: Finding equipment finance lenders for a two-year-old café without property to offer as security, comparing invoice finance options for a business with steady monthly revenue.*
- [Bazaro](https://bazaro.se) **`C`** - Connects to a restaurant's back office to search and approve supplier invoices, analyze spend and price changes, build and cost recipes, record waste, run stock counts, send purchase orders and contracts, and report sales per article, asking for confirmation before any change to data, on a Bazaro subscription with the AI module enabled. *Use case: Approving pending invoices from a fish supplier, recosting a burger recipe after a beef price increase, comparing weekly sales per menu item before a seasonal menu change.*
- [Brex](https://www.brex.com) - Reads Brex cards, expenses, spending limits and the expense policy, checks reimbursement payout dates and looks up users by name or email. *Use case: Checking when a reimbursement will be paid out, asking whether an expense fits the expense policy, reviewing spending limits.*
- [Clara](https://www.clara.com) **`C`** - Connects to a corporate card and spend management platform used by finance teams in Latin America. *Use case: Reviewing corporate card spending, answering finance-team questions about company expenses.*
- [Cortena FinOps](https://www.cortena.ai) **`C`** - Lists and opens Cortena payable invoices by due date, payment status, workflow step and supplier, checks approval and review queues, reads Pulse dashboard metrics and workflow definitions, and, with write access, approves, reviews or rejects invoices only after confirmation of a prepared change. *Use case: Checking which invoices need approval this week, finding overdue unpaid payables for a supplier, approving an invoice after reviewing it.*
- [Driversnote](https://www.driversnote.com) **`C`** - Lists, creates and classifies trips in a Driversnote mileage log, creates, previews and downloads mileage reports, submits reports to a team, and lists vehicles, locations, organisations, team members and team reports. *Use case: Classifying last month's trips, generating a mileage report and submitting it to a team.*
- [ExpenseBot](https://www.expensebot.ai) **`C`** - Reads receipt photos and PDFs into a Google Sheet with vendor, amount, date, tax and category, sweeps Gmail for unlogged receipts, logs cash expenses, mileage and income, runs spending and P&L analytics, and builds, shares and exports expense reports, read-only by default with granular write scopes. *Use case: Sweeping Gmail for receipts not yet logged, checking spending with one vendor last month, building an expense report to share with an accountant.*
- [Expensify](https://www.expensify.com) - Searches Expensify expenses, reports, reimbursements, receipts, trips, chats, tasks and invoices across workspaces to answer questions about spending trends. *Use case: Looking up a specific expense, reviewing spending trends, summarizing costs.*
- [Financy](https://financy.open-finance.ai) **`C`** - Reads Israeli bank, card and bit wallet data through Financy Open Banking, with balances, transactions and cash flow, and drafts and issues invoices, receipts and credit notes after approval. *Use case: Checking business cash flow across two banks this quarter, drafting a tax invoice for a client.*
- [Finom](https://finom.co) **`C`** - Reads business transactions and spending across wallets, invoices by customer, status or date, card details and benefits, bookkeeping entries, VAT categorization, and tax reporting deadlines and filing status for SMEs and freelancers in Europe. *Use case: Totaling software spending last quarter, finding unpaid invoices for a customer, checking the next VAT filing deadline.*
- [firmfact](https://firmfact.com) **`C`** - Answers questions about market data vendor contracts, invoices, cost allocations, subscriptions and utilisation stored in a firmfact workspace, analyzes cost trends, and hands broad questions to the workspace chat. *Use case: Checking which cost centre carries the Bloomberg terminal spend, finding underused LSEG subscriptions before a renewal, reviewing how FactSet costs changed over two years.*
- [Found](https://found.com) **`C`** - Checks self-employed business balances and pockets, pulls transactions by date or counterparty, and answers questions about business activity. *Use case: Checking how much is set aside for taxes, listing payments from one client, reviewing last month's spending.*
- [FundBiz Lender Index](https://fundbiz.co.uk) **`C`** - Searches the FundBiz Lender Index of UK business finance lenders by text, product type, soft search, limited-company-only or FCA-regulated flags, with published ticket ranges, rate bands and decision times, read-only. *Use case: Finding asset finance lenders that use a soft search for a limited company needing 40,000 pounds.*
- [Grasshopper Bank](https://www.grasshopper.bank) - Lists Grasshopper Bank accounts and balances, transactions, documents, external accounts, products and scheduled transfers, and analyzes spending patterns and financial trends. *Use case: Reviewing last month's transactions to spot spending patterns, checking upcoming scheduled transfers and account balances.*
- [Jeeves](https://tryjeeves.com) **`C`** - Reads company accounts, cards, transactions, credit and cash statements, bill pay invoices, reimbursements, vendors, policies and users across a global Jeeves spend program, and exports transactions to CSV. *Use case: Listing card transactions over 5000 dollars from last month, checking the latest credit statement balance and due date, finding reimbursements still pending approval.*
- [Kikin](https://www.kikin.io) **`C`** - Returns available funding, facility utilisation, loans and upcoming repayments from a Kikin working-capital account. *Use case: Checking how much of a credit facility is still available before paying a supplier, listing repayments due this month.*
- [Klisten Spend Recovery](https://klisten.co.kr/products.html) **`C`** - Audits supplied invoice, purchase-order, acknowledgement and contract rows for recoverable spend, builds evidence packets for disputes, records local review approvals on individual lines, and produces monthly savings reports, without contacting vendors or moving money. *Use case: Auditing a batch of supplier invoices against contract prices, building a dispute packet for an overbilled line, summarizing the month's detected savings.*
- [Libeo](https://libeo.io) **`C`** - Reads accounts-payable data from Libeo, a French finance platform, including invoices, credit notes, purchase orders, contracts, suppliers, documents awaiting approval or accounting review, bank and card transactions, budgets and accounting setup, with read-only access. *Use case: Listing supplier invoices waiting for business approval, checking how much of a department budget is consumed, finding unused credit notes from a supplier.*
- [Lili](https://lili.co) **`C`** - Searches and exports transactions, reads invoices, bills and account summaries, downloads statements and tax statements, generates expense reports and estimates taxes for a Lili business account, or across client accounts for accountants. *Use case: Checking business banking balances, reviewing recent transactions, managing client accounts for bookkeeping.*
- [Loot](https://getloot.com) **`C`** - Reads a business line of credit's available credit, upcoming payments with dates and amounts, and recent draws and payments, read-only. *Use case: Checking available credit before a large inventory purchase, confirming the date and amount of the next payment.*
- [Market Invoice Rate Index](https://marketinvoice.co.uk) **`C`** - Searches UK invoice finance providers from the Market Invoice Rate Index by text, category and terms, returning advance rate, service charge, minimum turnover, setup speed and an editorial rating. *Use case: Finding recruitment specialist factoring providers that accept 200,000 pounds turnover, comparing advance rates of high-street lenders.*
- [Memo Bank](https://memo.bank/communique-de-presse/avec-son-connecteur-mcp-memo-bank-permet-a-ses-clients-d-interroger-leurs-donnees-directement-depuis-leur-assistant-ia/) **`C`** - Retrieves real-time Memo Bank balances and IBANs, searches and filters transactions, and reads or uploads invoices and receipts as attachments on matching transactions, without executing payments. *Use case: Tracing an unexplained debit from last Tuesday, attaching a supplier invoice PDF to its matching transfer, checking balances across accounts before month-end close.*
- [Mendel](https://mendel.com) **`C`** - Searches corporate transactions, expenses pending audit, budgets, cost centers, people and cards for companies in Latin America, approves or rejects expenses and budget requests, creates and updates budgets, members, spend limits and restrictions, and approves, declines or cancels travel requests. *Use case: Approving a batch of pending expenses, raising a team member's monthly spend limit, declining an out-of-policy travel request.*
- [Mercury](https://mercury.com) - Reads Mercury accounts, transactions, cash flow, cards, statements, invoices, recipients, treasury and journal entries, categorizes and updates transactions, and sets up payments and transfers as requests for approval in Mercury. *Use case: Categorizing transactions, reviewing a cash flow summary, setting up a payment to a recipient for approval.*
- [Mesh](https://meshpayments.com) **`C`** - Expense management, cards, and travel through natural language. *Use case: Issuing and managing corporate cards, automating expense-report processing, booking business travel.*
- [mybanx](https://mybanx.io) **`C`** - Provides read-only access to business bank accounts at European banks connected through PSD2 open banking, listing companies, accounts with balances and transactions, and running read-only SQL queries on transactions. *Use case: Listing last month's outgoing payments over 1,000 euros, totaling incoming payments by client.*
- [Navan](https://navan.com) - Searches and reads expense transactions, receipts, cards, purchase cards, policies, users and spend summaries, updates transactions including approvals and rejections, attaches receipts and lists travel bookings in Navan. *Use case: Reviewing a team's spend for the quarter, approving a pending expense, attaching a receipt to a card transaction.*
- [Payhawk](https://payhawk.com) - Reads expenses, cards, budgets, balances, suppliers and close blockers, submits expenses and receipts, requests cards, approves or returns expenses, recodes items, manages suppliers, purchase orders and spend policies, runs exports and answers spend analytics queries. *Use case: Submitting a taxi receipt as an expense, approving expenses waiting in a review queue, finding close blockers before month-end.*
- [Pemo](https://www.pemo.io) **`C`** - Reads Pemo transactions, cards, team members, reimbursements and organizations, filtering, grouping and summarizing results, read-only with nothing created, approved or modified. *Use case: Looking up a specific transaction, listing a team member's reimbursements, grouping card spend by team member.*
- [platform.eved.com](https://eved.com) **`C`** - Provides access to the Eved platform, an accounts-payable and payments service used by event, film and music productions. *Use case: Checking whether a vendor invoice for a live event has been paid, reviewing supplier payments before a production closes its books, looking up a cross-border payout.*
- [Pleo](https://www.pleo.io) **`C`** - Searches and analyzes Pleo expenses, fixes incomplete ones in bulk, attaches receipts, applies categories and cost-centre tags, adds attendees, splits expenses, reviews pending approvals and queues expenses for export, confirming before writes. *Use case: Finding every expense missing a receipt before month-end export, tagging a backlog of travel costs to the right cost centre, adding colleagues as attendees on a client dinner.*
- [Pliant](https://www.getpliant.com) - Analyzes corporate card spending, transactions, merchants and account activity, finds receipts, categorizes, codes and splits transactions, maintains suppliers, projects, categories and VAT rates, updates card limits and controls, and invites members and manages teams and budgets, applying changes only after approval, with card requests and limit changes following the organization's approval rules. *Use case: Finding the top-spending cards this quarter, splitting a hotel charge across two projects, raising a team member's card limit for a trade show.*
- [Primary Treasury](https://primary.tech) **`C`** - Read-only queries against Primary financial data covering balances, transaction history, FX rates, accounts, and treasury reporting. *Use case: Checking balances across accounts this morning, pulling a month of transactions for reconciliation, looking up the FX rate applied to a transfer.*
- [Programscape](https://programscape.com) **`C`** - Searches and compares a sourced catalog of startup cloud and AI credits, accelerator programs and product perks, builds a review of programs worth pursuing, and saves plans that track changed terms. *Use case: Finding which cloud credit programs fit a seed-stage AI startup, comparing two accelerator offers side by side, checking whether a saved program changed its terms this month.*
- [PuraFi Expenses](https://www.expenseos.com/expenses-mcp) **`C`** - Turn receipts and drives into finished expense reports in chat — IRS-rate mileage, PDF/Excel/QuickBooks exports, one-click manager approval, and reimbursement tracking. *Use case: Snapping a photo of a restaurant receipt, logging a client-visit drive for mileage reimbursement, exporting the month's expense report to QuickBooks before payroll.*
- [Qonto](https://qonto.com) - Browses Qonto transactions and statements, manages clients, products and quotes, creates, sends and tracks client invoices and credit notes, reviews supplier invoices, manages debit, virtual and flash cards and approves or declines team expense requests, with the same access the person has in the Qonto app. *Use case: Issuing and sending a client invoice, reviewing pending supplier invoices, approving or declining a team expense request.*
- [Ramp](https://ramp.com) - Loads Ramp spend exports, purchase orders, cards, limits, vendors, departments, users, memos and spend programs into tables and runs queries over them to analyze company spend. *Use case: Breaking down spend by department and vendor, checking card limits, reviewing purchase orders.*
- [Receiptor AI](https://receiptor.ai) **`C`** - Searches, inspects and summarizes receipts, bills and invoices from email, WhatsApp, uploads and connected inboxes, and edits, deduplicates and deletes documents, merges merchants and entities, approves or rejects items, runs rules, sends to integrations and creates exports and views. *Use case: Locating a specific receipt from last quarter, organizing documents by vendor, generating a visual summary of spending.*
- [Relay Financial](https://relayfi.com) **`C`** - Reads Relay business banking data in read-only mode, including balances across checking and savings accounts, transaction history filtered by account, card, date or amount, card spend by cardholder, invoices, contacts, catalog items and tax rates, and lists monthly statements with PDF download links, without moving money or changing accounts. *Use case: Comparing vendor spend month over month across two operating accounts, breaking down team card spend by cardholder, downloading last quarter's bank statements for the bookkeeper.*
- [Rho](https://www.rho.co) **`C`** - Reads Rho accounts, transactions, cards, cash flow, credit programs, expenses, reimbursements, invoices and invoicing customers, statements, money movements and declined authorizations, with their attached files. *Use case: Reviewing business banking transactions, modeling cash flow, analyzing spend.*
- [Rouliva](https://rouliva.eu) **`C`** - Lists and creates Rouliva expenses, trips and projects, and returns account summaries of out-of-pocket spending and mileage. *Use case: Checking which expenses this month are missing receipts and logging a 120 km client trip.*
- [Round Treasury](https://roundtreasury.com) **`C`** - Pulls Round Treasury account balances and bank transactions along with connected Xero accounts, bank transactions, invoices, balance sheets and profit and loss reports. *Use case: Checking cash across accounts before approving a large supplier payment, pulling last quarter's Xero profit and loss, listing Xero invoices for a cash forecast.*
- [Rydoo](https://www.rydoo.com) **`C`** - Searches Rydoo expenses and trips the user owns or oversees, finds card expenses missing receipts, answers expense-policy questions, approves or rejects expenses after confirming each action, and, with the Rydoo Cards module, reviews cards, wallets and statements and requests virtual cards with a limit the user confirms. *Use case: Approving the expenses waiting in a queue, checking whether a dinner is reimbursable under policy, finding card expenses still missing a receipt.*
- [SaaS RFP](https://saasrfp.com) **`C`** - Posts public RFPs for paid software tools, searches vendors, RFPs and products, reads RFP bids with prices, savings and per-feature match percentages, lets sellers create products and submit bids, and lets buyers shortlist, reject or accept bids and close RFPs. *Use case: Posting RFPs for a company's CRM and design tools in one call, comparing the bids on the CRM RFP by feature match.*
- [SaaSI Hub](https://www.saasihub.com) **`C`** - Reads SaaSi Hub data on company software, including applications, seats, spend summaries and trends, idle licences, upcoming renewals, risky apps and leavers who still have access. *Use case: Finding former employees who still hold paid seats and which renewals are due next month.*
- [Sealgraf](https://sealgraf.com) **`C`** - Checks an agent's proposed purchase against spending limits set by the user, asking for approval above the threshold, and retrieves the sealed receipt for a finished purchase. *Use case: Letting an agent buy a 12-dollar domain renewal on its own while a 300-dollar software license waits for a tap, pulling the receipt for last week's purchase.*
- [Shine Business Account](https://www.shine.fr) **`C`** - Reads Shine business account balances, transactions, transfers, invoices, quotes, receipts and company information. *Use case: Checking the cash position before paying suppliers, listing the biggest expenses this quarter, preparing a summary for the accountant.*
- [Skipo](https://skipo.com) **`C`** - Read-only access to your Skipo account — check balances, transactions, deposits, withdrawals, and conversions in natural language, right inside Claude. *Use case: Checking today's account balance before a transfer, confirming a recent deposit cleared, converting a foreign currency balance to see its current value.*
- [SMB Compare](https://smbcompare.com) **`C`** - Ranks payment providers, card machines, POS, payroll, accounting, CRM and other business software by estimated monthly cost for Australia, the UK and the US, using published provider pricing. *Use case: Comparing card processing costs for a UK cafe taking 15,000 pounds a month in card sales, ranking US payroll providers for a 12-person team, checking which POS systems cost least for a small Australian retailer.*
- [Spendflo](https://www.spendflo.com) **`C`** - Looks up Spendflo purchase requests and their approvers, vendors, contracts, upcoming renewals, purchase orders, workflows, users, departments and teams, and summarizes contracts and renewals to support procurement decisions. *Use case: Checking which SaaS renewals are coming up, looking up a vendor's contract before a negotiation, finding who needs to approve a pending purchase request.*
- [Spendify](https://spendify.money) **`C`** - Shows bank balances and balance history, searches and filters transactions, breaks down spending by category, merchant, day and month, manages budgets, categories, tags and automation rules, and tracks recurring charges, for bank accounts synced through Plaid. *Use case: Comparing monthly spending against category budgets, categorizing uncategorized transactions, reviewing upcoming recurring charges.*
- [Stackcut](https://stackcut.io) **`C`** - Audits software subscriptions and whole stacks against a catalog of published vendor plan prices, finds cheaper plans, alternatives and build-it-yourself paths, and estimates costs for a planned product at a given scale. *Use case: Checking for something cheaper than a $100-a-month Typeform plan, auditing Notion and Zapier spend for a 12-person team, pricing auth and Postgres for 10,000 monthly users.*
- [SubCenter for Teams](https://subcenter.io/teams) **`C`** - Reports SaaS and AI-tool spend, upcoming renewals, cancel-by dates, seats and invoices in a SubCenter for Teams workspace, and adds, cancels or keeps tools, assigns seats, manages clients, people and bill-back reports, asking first before anything that deletes or offboards. *Use case: Listing renewals due in the next 30 days with cancel-by dates, assigning a design tool seat to a new designer, building a client bill-back report for October.*
- [Swypex](https://www.swypex.com) **`C`** - Answers questions about company spend, corporate cards, invoices, customers, and suppliers from an Egyptian business finance account, and adjusts card spend limits. *Use case: Explaining why expenses rose this month, ranking suppliers by amount paid last quarter, raising a team card's spend limit.*
- [SyntheticFi](https://www.syntheticfi.com) **`C`** - Reads SyntheticFi advisor client accounts, box-spread loan segments, collateral, LTV, margin cushion, rate quotes, restrictions, transactions, upcoming expirations, repayment options and documents. *Use case: Checking how much a client can borrow before a home purchase, listing loans expiring next month.*
- [TopFunders Business Funding](https://topfunders.ai) **`C`** - Estimates which funding types fit a US small business from its revenue, time in business and credit range, with approximate borrowing ranges, typical costs, loan payments and merchant cash advance costs. *Use case: Estimating how much a two-year-old trucking company could borrow, comparing the daily cost of a $40k advance at a 1.3 factor rate, calculating monthly payments on an equipment loan.*
- [TraxAgent](https://www.traxagent.io) **`C`** - Reports AI spend across model APIs, coding tools and assistant seats by team, person, model and API key, and lists cost-saving fixes with their monthly savings. *Use case: Breaking down last week's AI spend by team, finding which API key caused Tuesday's cost jump.*
- [TreasurySpring](https://treasuryspring.com) **`C`** - Read-only access to TreasurySpring portfolio holdings, upcoming maturities, available fixed-term funds, and portfolio reports. *Use case: Checking which positions mature in the next 30 days, comparing available funds before a cash allocation, pulling a quarter-end holdings report.*
- [Trio](https://trio.com.br) **`C`** - Reads consolidated balances, statements and bank transfers from a Trio corporate account, traces Pix payments and receivables, validates Pix keys, and reviews corporate card purchases and invoices, read-only. *Use case: Tracing which Pix payment matches a customer's receipt, totaling this month's corporate card spend by card.*
- [Tropic](https://www.tropicapp.io) - Benchmarks software and AI pricing against $18B+ in verified technology transactions, pulls supplier intelligence and negotiation playbooks, queries contract documents, and finds redundant spend. *Use case: Checking whether a software contract is overpriced against benchmarks, pulling a negotiation playbook for a supplier, finding redundant spend across contracts.*
- [Vendr Software Pricing Tools](https://www.vendr.com) - Searches Vendr's company and software product catalog, returns custom price estimates and provides negotiation insights for software purchases. *Use case: Getting a price estimate for a software product before buying, preparing negotiation points for a software deal.*
- [Vivid MCP](https://vivid.money/en-eu/business) **`C`** - Builds a pre-filled onboarding link for a Vivid Business account from company details or uploaded registration documents, leaving identity verification and account creation to the person. *Use case: Turning company registration documents into a pre-filled Vivid Business onboarding link, starting an application without retyping company details.*
- [Weel](https://letsweel.com) **`C`** - Reads business transactions, budgets and budget members, approvals, bills, reimbursements, suppliers, employees, categories, custom fields and tax rates from a business spend account for analysis. *Use case: Reviewing spend against the marketing budget this quarter, listing reimbursements awaiting approval, finding all bills from one supplier.*
- [Zip](https://zip.com) **`C`** - Looks up purchase requests with approval status and audit trails, pending approvals, vendors, agreements, purchase orders, invoices, credit memos and catalog items, and browses departments, locations, GL accounts, categories, workflow configurations and intake forms. *Use case: Checking who still needs to approve a software purchase request, pulling every open purchase order for a vendor, seeing how a new laptop request gets routed.*

## Credit Risk Compliance and Insurance

- [21RISK](https://21risk.com) **`C`** - Queries and updates risk and compliance data for industrial sites, including audits, risk improvement actions, claims and insured values, and searches 21RISK product documentation. *Use case: Listing sites with open actions from the last audit, totaling insured value across German sites, marking a sprinkler inspection action as compliant.*
- [Addy AI](https://addy.com) **`C`** - Searches a mortgage loan pipeline, opens loan details, processing checklists, underwriting reviews and lender guidelines, lists missing documents, requests them from borrowers by email or text after approval, and creates borrowers and loan files. *Use case: Finding which documents are still missing on a refinance file, texting a borrower for two recent pay stubs, checking whether last week's bank statement request was answered.*
- [AIR Credit Intelligence](https://www.airplatforms.com) **`C`** - Looks up company entities, returns machine-learning credit ratings with explanations of their drivers, and runs credit scenarios to test how ratings respond to future conditions. *Use case: Assessing a supplier's credit rating before signing a contract, understanding what drives a counterparty's downgrade, testing a revenue-decline scenario on a borrower.*
- [Aoibhe](https://www.aibroker.ie) **`C`** - Answers questions about an insurance brokerage's clients, policies, cover, renewals, tasks, client conversations and regulatory deadlines from Aoibhe data, read-only. *Use case: Listing motor policies due for renewal next month, finding clients with missing contact details before a regulatory deadline.*
- [ARAG Spain Insurance Products](https://www.arag.es/productos/defensa-juridica/alquiler) - Explains ARAG Home Rental insurance for landlords in Spain, gives an indicative annual price and returns ARAG contact details. *Use case: Getting an indicative yearly price to insure a flat let in Valencia at 900 euros a month, checking what the cover includes.*
- [Ask the Clock (Ctrl Worx Watch List)](https://ctrlworx.com) **`C`** - Looks up whether a public company is on the Ctrl Worx Watch List for an open material weakness reported in SEC filings, how long it has been open, and the source filing, and returns list totals. *Use case: Checking whether a ticker on a shortlist has an open material weakness, counting how many companies are on the Clock this week.*
- [AuditCake](https://auditcake.com) **`C`** - Lists and reads insurance premium audits in AuditCake, with status, timelines, carriers, exposure totals and redacted documents, read-only. *Use case: Listing audits due this month for one carrier, totalling payroll exposure across open audits, reading a redacted audit document.*
- [BerniePortal](https://www.bernieportal.com) **`C`** - Answers questions about a benefits broker's BerniePortal book of business, including employer clients, renewals, offered plans, premium grids, enrollment and elections, and benefits documents. *Use case: Listing employer clients renewing in January, comparing health plan enrollment across a book of business, pulling a client's plan documents.*
- [Buckingham Licence Register](https://www.buckinghamcapitalconsulting.com) **`C`** - Checks whether a payment, e-money, crypto, money transfer, lending or financial services firm is licensed, warned about or fined, from regulators' registers, and lists coverage. *Use case: Checking whether a crypto exchange holds a licence before onboarding it, verifying an EU e-money licence.*
- [Business ID Check](https://bizid.tidybutton.com) **`C`** - Validates EU and UK VAT numbers, Australian and New Zealand business numbers, LEIs and other tax identifiers singly or in batches and shows check history. *Use case: Validating 200 supplier VAT numbers before a payment run.*
- [CardoAI ABF MCP](https://cardoai.com) **`C`** - Query asset-backed finance portfolio analytics and stratification data across your authorized CardoAI securitization transactions. *Use case: Checking the stratification breakdown of a securitization pool by credit rating, pulling portfolio analytics before reporting to investors.*
- [Certgio](https://certgio.com) **`C`** - Checks a US certificate of insurance against a contract's insurance requirements, limit by limit, and explains common construction endorsements such as CG 20 10 and waiver of subrogation. *Use case: Checking a roofing subcontractor's COI against a 2 million dollar aggregate requirement, explaining what CG 20 37 covers.*
- [CertifiKit COI Tools](https://certifikit.ai/mcp-server) **`C`** - Builds certificate-of-insurance requirements for subcontractors and vendors with limits, endorsements and contract exhibit text, checks an ACORD 25 certificate against requirements with pass, fail or needs-review results, and lists presets and guides. *Use case: Setting insurance limits for a roofing subcontractor, checking a vendor's ACORD 25 for an expired policy, generating exhibit text for a contract.*
- [Claim Ready](https://claimreadyapp.com) **`C`** - Reads coverage-gap findings, dwelling-limit shortfalls, risk summaries and policy coverage summaries from completed Claim Ready reviews for authorized households, on a paid Claim Ready advisor or agent account. *Use case: Preparing for a client meeting by listing the coverage gaps found in a household's homeowners policy.*
- [Clarity AI](https://clarity.ai) **`C`** - Sustainability data and SFDR fund classification. *Use case: ESG reporting, SFDR 2.0 fund categorization, sustainable investment disclosures.*
- [Clearparty](https://app.clearparty.dev) **`C`** - Resolves a company name, LEI or VAT number to its GLEIF legal entity and parent chain, screens it against EU, UN, UK and US OFAC sanctions lists, and validates EU VAT numbers in VIES. *Use case: Checking a new German supplier's VAT number before paying an invoice, screening a counterparty's parent company against sanctions lists.*
- [Cognitive Credit](https://cognitivecredit.com) **`C`** - Real-time credit data, analytics, and AI-driven insights across public and private bond and loan markets. *Use case: Pull real-time bond market analytics on a company's credit profile before drafting an investment memo.*
- [ConditionPilot](https://conditionpilot.com) **`C`** - Processes uploaded lender condition sheets into per-loan checklists, borrower drafts and a queue ranked by rate-lock expiry, with job status, results and credit balance. *Use case: Turning a stack of condition sheets into loan checklists, prioritizing files closest to rate-lock expiry.*
- [Contezza AI for Financial Services](https://contezza.ai/financial-services.html) **`C`** - Answers lending and regulatory questions from curated US federal sources with citations and effective dates, screens lending policies against regulation, benchmarks mortgage applications, analyzes rent rolls and builds prequalification and commercial worksheets. *Use case: Checking when the first Section 1071 compliance date applies, screening a small business lending policy against fair lending rules.*
- [CredCore - Tusk Liquid](https://credcore.com) **`C`** - Curated database of public credit-agreement terms covering covenants, debt structure, pricing, and amendments. *Use case: Researching covenant language across deals, comparing debt structures, tracking amendment history.*
- [CreditPolicy.ai](https://creditpolicy.ai) **`C`** - Searches current Australian lender credit policy documents with the lender and document named for each figure, manages broker client files, deals and scenarios, runs multi-lender servicing, and sends client fact-find portals. *Use case: Checking which lenders accept a self-employed borrower with one year of tax returns, running servicing for a couple across three lenders, sending a fact-find portal to a new client.*
- [Criteria by Lendmind](https://lendmind.com) **`C`** - Returns UK mortgage lenders' published lending criteria as quoted passages with page links, maps a case description to criteria topics, and compares which lenders set conditions or exclusions on them. *Use case: Finding lenders that accept a contractor with six months' history, comparing buy-to-let criteria for first-time landlords, quoting a lender's policy on gifted deposits.*
- [D&B Finance Analytics](https://www.dnb.com/en-us/products/dnb-credit-intelligence.html) - Credit origination and workflow tools built on Dun and Bradstreet business data. *Use case: Running a credit decision on a new customer, automating a credit origination workflow, monitoring portfolio risk.*
- [D&B Risk Analytics](https://www.dnb.com/products/dnb-supplier-intelligence.html) - Supplier intelligence and third-party risk monitoring from the Dun & Bradstreet Data Cloud. *Use case: Supplier screening, ongoing risk monitoring, compliance reporting, supply-chain due diligence powered by the commercial graph.*
- [DateBPI](https://www.datebpi.ro) **`C`** - Searches companies in Romania by name or CUI and returns sourced ONRC and ANAF profiles with arrears and early risk signals, reads Insolvency Proceedings Bulletin (BPI) publications, creditor tables, auctions, creditor meetings and linked companies, gives claim-filing deadlines, and adds companies to a Radar watchlist with alerts on new publications. *Use case: Checking whether a Romanian supplier has an open insolvency case before extending credit, finding the deadline to file a claim against a debtor, adding three customers to the Radar for new BPI alerts.*
- [Evix Lab — Regulatory Watch](https://evixlab.com) **`C`** - Lists changes from the last 90 days to AML/CFT laws, regulations, supervisory guidance and drafts from watched authorities, by authority or kind of change, and opens each change with its changed provisions, was-and-became wording where quotable, date in force and official source link. *Use case: Checking what changed in FINTRAC guidance this month, reviewing new drafts from the Bank of Lithuania, listing which authorities the feed watches.*
- [Fitch Solutions](https://www.fitchsolutions.com) - Queries ratings, financials, key rating drivers, peers and research from Fitch Ratings, and CreditSights articles, business profiles, credit views, core scores, key metrics, event risk and deal tranches. *Use case: Pulling an issuer's ratings and key rating drivers, comparing it with peers, reading CreditSights' credit view on a company.*
- [Gentek Reg Agent](https://www.gentekai.com/mcp-server) **`C`** - Regulatory reporting intelligence across global regimes, grounded in official source material. *Use case: Checking a specific country's reporting requirement before a filing deadline, verifying a rule against its official source, comparing reporting regimes across two jurisdictions.*
- [Global Asset Management Standards](https://open.gamstandards.com) **`C`** - Reads the GAMS open library of asset-management standards, such as questionnaires, document templates, JSON data schemas, glossary terms and FAQs, and searches and answers across collections with citations after sign-in. *Use case: Pulling the fund data schema fields to extract data from a prospectus, reading the due-diligence questionnaire question bank.*
- [Global Asset Management Standards Publishers](https://gamstandards.com) **`C`** - Works in the GAMS Publisher Console with the member's permissions, reading working-group actions, threads and documents, comparing versions, reviewing collections and metrics, and drafting FAQs, terms, updates and document edits. *Use case: Catching up on a working group's open actions before Tuesday's call, comparing two versions of a draft template.*
- [iDenfy](https://idenfy.com) **`C`** - Reports aggregated identity (KYC), business (KYB) and AML verification statistics, looks up a single verification by reference, lists KYB companies, flows, questionnaires and automation rules, reviews webhook deliveries, and reads or updates account settings. *Use case: Comparing KYC denial reasons by country this quarter, checking the status of a flagged verification, reviewing failed webhook deliveries.*
- [Insolvenzmonitor](https://insolvenzmonitor.online) **`C`** - Checks German companies against official insolvency notices with proceeding stage, case number and court, reads a company's notice history, screens customer and supplier lists, and returns insolvency statistics. *Use case: Checking whether a Munich supplier has opened insolvency proceedings, screening 300 customers before a credit run.*
- [Insuraviews](https://insuraviews.com) **`C`** - Searches and counts US P&C insurance rate and rule filings by carrier, state and line, reads filing details and supporting documents, and resolves carrier and group names. *Use case: Finding homeowners rate filings in Texas this year, reading a carrier's latest auto rate change exhibit.*
- [Insurers Global](https://www.kenbright.ai) **`C`** - Searches a directory of licensed insurers, reinsurers, and takaful operators across 160 markets, with regulator, class of business, and ownership group. *Use case: Listing life insurers licensed in Ghana, identifying the insurance regulator in Mauritius, tracing which entities hold licences for an insurance group.*
- [Integrated AI](https://www.integrated-ai.com) **`C`** - Drafts AI governance policies, checks text for PII and PHI before AI use, assesses AI readiness, finds AI workflows for insurance agencies and benefits brokerages, and gives Integrated AI pricing and contacts. *Use case: Drafting an AI use policy for a 12-person benefits brokerage, checking a client email for health data before pasting it.*
- [IntelliCompli](https://www.intellicompli.com.au) **`C`** - Screens customers against sanctions and PEP lists, assesses and explains customer and transaction risk, manages alerts and enhanced due diligence investigations, and generates TTR and IFTI regulatory reports for AML/CTF compliance. *Use case: Screening a new corporate client and its beneficial owners against sanctions lists, resolving a typology alert on a large cash deposit, generating a threshold transaction report for the week.*
- [Kennek](https://kennek.io) **`C`** - Searches Kennek lending enquiries, applications, checklists, risk assessments, loans, repayment schedules, transactions, redemption figures and portfolio reports, and adds notes, tasks and risk answers. *Use case: Reviewing the repayment schedule of a bridging loan, creating a follow-up task for an application missing valuation documents.*
- [KYB Vetter](https://kybvetter.dev) **`C`** - Verifies a Polish company by NIP against the VAT white list and KRS register, checks whether a bank account is assigned to it, and reads the history of checks, each with a receipt. *Use case: Checking a new supplier's VAT status and bank account before paying a 20 000 PLN invoice.*
- [Market Tracker: DOT & Trucking Carrier Data](https://www.nativebase.ai) **`C`** - Looks up US motor carriers by USDOT or MC number from live FMCSA data, including operating authority, fleet, insurer on file, and estimated renewal date. *Use case: Checking whether a trucking prospect's authority is active, seeing who writes a carrier's insurance, building a call list of upcoming renewal dates.*
