- [mcp.vectelos.com](https://vectelos.com) **`C`** - Looks up tariff lines and computes US import duty per line or entry from an HTS code, origin and customs value, stacking MFN rates with Section 301 and 232 remedies plus MPF and HMF, citing the schedule revision used. *Use case: Estimating duty on a shipment of Chinese-origin steel fasteners, checking which Section 301 rate stacks on a specific HTS line, confirming which schedule revision a duty estimate was computed from.*
- [MGT Quoting](https://mgtinsurance.com) - Classifies a small business, checks it against MGT's commercial insurance appetite, and creates, edits and validates quotes for licensed insurance agents. *Use case: Quoting a pizzeria described in plain language, checking whether a roofing contractor fits MGT's appetite.*
- [Middesk](https://docs.middesk.com) **`C`** - Orders, retrieves and searches business verification reports, creating businesses and orders, listing enabled packages, searching business registrations and creating and listing risk signals. *Use case: Ordering a verification report on a new vendor, searching registrations for a company name, reviewing risk signals on onboarded businesses.*
- [Moody's Analytics](https://www.moodys.com) - Credit risk analytics and economic forecasting. *Use case: Fixed income analysis, credit risk assessment, economic scenario modeling.*
- [Moody's Credit MCP](https://www.moodys.com/web/en/us/capabilities/credit-risk.html) - Finds companies and pulls their Moody's ratings, credit opinion summaries and outlooks, upgrade and downgrade factors, scorecards, peers, SWOT, ESG, sector outlooks and country profiles, and searches earnings calls, documents and news. *Use case: Checking a company's risk profile before a decision, comparing risk analytics across two options, pulling insights to support a recommendation.*
- [Morningstar Credit Analytics](https://credit.morningstar.com) - Searches structured credit deals and loans, reads deal details, loans, bonds, loss forecasts, commentary, noteworthy metrics, breakdowns and surveillance dashboards, searches loan newsflashes and analyzes portfolios. *Use case: Reviewing a deal's top loans and loss forecast, searching loan newsflashes, analyzing a portfolio.*
- [PerilScore](https://app.perilscore.com/developers/) **`C`** - Scores US addresses for catastrophe risk, fire protection, crime and construction details, and builds underwriting reports with COPE information, replacement-cost ranges and building-permit signals, plus exportable score factors and report links. *Use case: Scoring a coastal Florida address before referring a homeowners policy, building an underwriting report ahead of a commercial property inspection, exporting the factors behind a high catastrophe score.*
- [Peru RUC Check](https://taxid.halpii.com/peru-ruc-lookup) **`C`** - Validates a Peruvian RUC tax ID against SUNAT registry data and returns the legal name, status, fiscal-address condition, address and a pass, review or fail verdict. *Use case: Checking a Lima supplier's RUC before paying its first invoice.*
- [PlanVantage](https://www.planvantage.ai) **`C`** - Builds employee benefits plan-year dashboards from monthly claims, projects renewals with stop-loss and credibility assumptions, compares plan designs by actuarial value, models contribution strategies, and assembles client exhibits. *Use case: Projecting a client's renewal with a specific stop-loss deductible, comparing a PPO against a proposed HDHP, modeling an 80/60 employer contribution split.*
- [Public Adjusters Directory](https://publicadjusters.fyi) **`C`** - Searches a directory of licensed public insurance adjusters, returning licence details, places, categories and the source behind each record. *Use case: Checking a public adjuster's licence before hiring, finding licensed adjusters by place, confirming where a licence record came from.*
- [QAML](https://qaml.quoqo.app) **`C`** - Screens people and companies against sanctions, export-control, politically exposed person and adverse media lists, reads screenings and open cases, proposes decisions and produces screening reports. *Use case: Screening a new corporate customer and its directors before onboarding and reading the screening report.*
- [Radicado Uno](https://radicadouno.co) **`C`** - Checks Colombian companies by NIT or name, returning business registry status, public contracts, procurement sanctions and OFAC or UN list matches with links to official sources. *Use case: Verifying a supplier's registration is active before signing, checking a contractor for procurement sanctions, finding a company's NIT.*
- [Rollee](https://developers.getrollee.com) **`C`** - Reads income, employment, transactions, trips, vehicles, profile details and documents that end users connected from ride-hailing, delivery, freelance and payroll platforms, creates and updates users, opens, refreshes and reconnects link sessions, disconnects accounts and configures event webhooks. *Use case: Reviewing an applicant's verified delivery-platform income, opening a reconnect session for a broken payroll link, checking which events a webhook listens to.*
- [Rollee for Companies](https://developers.getrollee.com/docs/mcp2) **`C`** - Reads companies' connected payroll, HR and fleet accounts, including employees, employment records, absences, reported income and payouts, performance, vehicles, trips, banking details and documents, registers companies, updates company users, opens, refreshes and reconnects link sessions, and configures event webhooks. *Use case: Finding which of a company's connected accounts stopped syncing and opening a reconnect session, checking an employee's absences and reported income, reviewing a fleet's trips for a quarter.*
- [Rotuli: UK company data](https://rotuli.co.uk) **`C`** - Looks up UK companies from Companies House and HM Land Registry data, returning profiles, officers, persons with significant control, ownership graphs, insolvency and risk signals for KYB checks. *Use case: Vetting a new supplier's directors and overdue filings, mapping who controls a holding company.*
- [Sanction Scanner](https://www.sanctionscanner.com) **`C`** - AML compliance for Claude — screen names, check adverse media, manage cases, and run compliance reports using your SanctionScanner account. *Use case: Screening a new vendor's name before onboarding, checking adverse media on a business partner, running a compliance report before a quarterly audit.*
- [SESAMm](https://www.sesamm.com) **`C`** - Screens public and private companies for ESG controversies and reputational risk drawn from news and web data, returning severity-scored cases, events and source documents with UN Global Compact and SDG statistics, builds and edits company universes with aggregate metrics, reports and exports, and runs text analytics and document translation. *Use case: Pulling the controversy history of a private company before an investment, comparing ESG case severity across a portfolio universe, reporting UN Global Compact alignment for a fund's holdings.*
- [Taktile](https://mcp-docs.taktile.com) - Builds, tests and explains Taktile automated decisions, manages agents, case queues, templates and cases, connections and audit events. *Use case: Creating a case for a flagged loan application, testing a new agent version in the playground.*
- [Topograph](https://topograph.co) **`C`** - Searches official business registers to verify company identity and status, retrieves directors, legal representatives, shareholders and beneficial owners where available, orders official documents, checks coverage and prices, and sets up monitoring. *Use case: Verifying a new supplier's registration status before onboarding, pulling beneficial owners for a counterparty review, checking document prices before ordering a register extract.*
- [Validis](https://www.validis.com) **`C`** - Pulls standardised general ledger, receivables, payables, and trial balance data from company accounting systems and runs ratio, aging, and fraud-indicator analytics. *Use case: Reviewing a borrower's receivables aging before a credit decision, checking ledger integrity flags during an audit, comparing financial ratios between two engagements.*
- [Verety.ai](https://www.verety.ai) **`C`** - Searches official business registries for companies by name, registration number or free text, reads registry records with legal name, status, address, officers and capital, and runs agent-led KYB investigations that return risk-scored reports covering checks such as sanctions, ownership and adverse media. *Use case: Vetting a new supplier before onboarding, confirming a counterparty's registry status and officers, turning an investigation report into a risk memo.*
- [Verika – Italian VAT, Tax Code & IBAN Check](https://github.com/JackPons/partita-iva-mcp) **`C`** - Validates Italian VAT numbers, tax codes and IBANs, checks live VAT status on VIES, and builds company profiles combining public administration, GLEIF and municipality data with an indicative EU sanctions screening. *Use case: Verifying a new Italian supplier's partita IVA before payment, validating an IBAN on an invoice, screening a company name against the EU sanctions list.*
- [Verisk Underwriting Intelligence](https://www.verisk.com/resources/verisk-underwriting-intelligence/) - ISO loss costs, indications, and experience-trend data for P&C insurers. *Use case: Plain-language queries against actuarial filings, indication-driver analysis, loss-cost trend research for underwriting and pricing teams.*
- [Verisk XactRestore](https://www.verisk.com/products/xactrestore/) - Builds XactRestore estimates from a description of the work, searching jobs and projects, creating projects, creating, updating, duplicating and archiving rooms or areas, and finding, pricing, adding, updating or archiving line items. *Use case: Adding rooms and line items to a restoration estimate from a description of the work, checking a line item's pricing, duplicating a room in a remodeling estimate.*
- [WhoWritesThis.co.uk](https://whowritesthis.co.uk/ai) **`C`** - Finds which UK insurers, MGAs and Lloyd's markets write a given trade, checks appetite with links to each market's own documents, looks up market details and reports recent changes. *Use case: Finding markets that write roofers with hot works, checking whether an MGA covers scaffolders.*
- [Zeitro](https://www.zeitro.com) **`C`** - Searches Zeitro's library of Fannie Mae, Freddie Mac, FHA, VA and USDA mortgage guidelines with document and page citations, and calculates DTI and FHA mortgage insurance. *Use case: Finding the FHA rule on gift funds for a down payment, calculating DTI for a 4,200 dollar monthly income.*
- [Zentria Flow](https://zentriaflow.com) **`C`** - Calculates current US import duty for an HTS code and country of origin with every tariff layer and dated changes, compares origins and searches HTSUS lines. *Use case: Pricing the duty on cotton T-shirts from China against Vietnam, finding the HTS code for steel brackets.*

## Crypto and Web3

- [0xArchive](https://0xarchive.io) **`C`** - Provides historical and real-time Hyperliquid and Lighter market data, including order-level books, trades, funding, open interest, and liquidations. *Use case: Reconstructing the order book at the moment of a liquidation cascade, pulling funding rates for a backtest, checking open interest around a market launch.*
- [Aperiodic - Market Data for RWA & Crypto](https://aperiodic.io) **`C`** - Returns point-in-time order flow, liquidity, derivatives and OHLCV metrics and raw trades and quotes for crypto and tokenized asset perpetuals on Binance, OKX and Hyperliquid, and describes datasets and symbols. *Use case: Comparing BTC perp funding and L2 depth on Binance and Hyperliquid during last Tuesday's sell-off, finding the tokenized gold perps listed on OKX.*
- [BackPoW](https://backpow.com) **`C`** - Returns live Proof-of-Work mining data, including difficulty, hashrate, hardware benchmarks, cost of production, solo-mining odds and mining news. *Use case: Calculating solo-mining odds for Bitcoin at 100 TH/s, checking whether Monero mining is profitable, comparing ASIC efficiency.*
- [Bitcoin Halving Cycles](https://www.bitcoinhalving.info) **`C`** - Returns Bitcoin's position in its four-year halving cycle against the 2012, 2016 and 2020 cycles on the same day after each halving, the next halving countdown, prices after halvings and past cycle highs and lows, read-only. *Use case: Comparing today's price multiple since the 2024 halving with the 2016 cycle, checking how many days remain to the next halving.*
- [Bitcoin Rainbow](https://thebitcoinrainbow.com) **`C`** - Returns today's Bitcoin Rainbow chart band, explains Bitcoin price history and simulates past weekly purchases. *Use case: Checking which band bitcoin is in today, replaying 50 dollars a week since 2020.*
- [Blockworks MCP](https://blockworks.com) **`C`** - Searches crypto and blockchain research, news and documents, and pulls tabular and time-series market data, fundamentals, signals, events and fundraising data from catalogs. *Use case: Pulling daily fee revenue for a layer-2 network over the past quarter, finding recent research notes on a token unlock, checking which crypto startups raised funding this month.*
- [Buildix: Hyperliquid Orderflow](https://www.buildix.trade) **`C`** - Returns read-only orderflow analytics for Hyperliquid perpetual markets, including buy and sell flow, order book imbalance, VPIN, funding and open interest, estimated liquidation levels, whale positioning and a market screener. *Use case: Checking where liquidations cluster around the ETH price, ranking markets by funding rate, seeing whether tracked whales are net long on BTC.*
- [Chain of Title](https://chainoftitle.org) **`C`** - Looks up Solana token launches recorded at their creation transaction, returning the archived name, ticker, description, links and image with hashes and citations, searching by ticker or name and reporting archive status. *Use case: Retrieving the original launch description of a token whose website went dark, checking when a ticker was first created on Solana.*
- [CoinDesk](https://data.coindesk.com) - Fetches live and historical digital asset spot, futures and options data from CoinDesk, including ticks, OHLCV, orderbooks, trades, open interest and funding rates, plus index values and crypto news. *Use case: Pulling historical OHLCV data for a futures contract, checking funding rates and open interest, comparing an index with spot prices.*
- [CoinGecko](https://www.coingecko.com) - Live cryptocurrency prices, comparisons, charts, and market context. *Use case: Checking Bitcoin's price before making a trade, comparing two altcoins' performance over the past month, pulling up a chart to see recent volatility.*
- [CoinLobster](https://www.coinlobster.com) **`C`** - Tracks large crypto trades across 18 exchanges, Hyperliquid and on-chain swaps on Ethereum, Base and Arbitrum, flags unusual buying or selling, and returns coin briefs, liquidations, merged order books, funding, open interest, options, news, top Hyperliquid accounts with positions and leverage, watchlists and chat-set alerts. *Use case: Asking why a coin jumped this morning, checking how close a large Hyperliquid account is to liquidation, setting an alert for unusual whale selling.*
- [CoinMarketCap](https://coinmarketcap.com) - Retrieves real-time cryptocurrency quotes, global market metrics, technical indicators, on-chain metrics, derivatives data, crypto news, trending narratives and upcoming macro events from CoinMarketCap. *Use case: Checking Bitcoin's RSI and MACD before a trade, reviewing funding rates and open interest after a liquidation spike, scanning which narratives are trending this week.*
- [Coinversa Pulse](https://coinversa.ai) - Read-only Hyperliquid intelligence covering wallet cohorts, trade history with PnL attribution, position lifecycles and live risk. *Use case: Profiling a wallet's trading behavior, reviewing liquidation risk across open positions, finding cohorts that are buying.*
- [Crypto.com](https://crypto.com) - Reads Crypto.com market data for cryptocurrency tokens, including price quotes, tickers, trades, mark and index prices, order books, instrument details and candlestick charts. *Use case: Checking the latest price of a token, pulling candlestick data for a chart, looking at the order book for an instrument.*
- [CryptoETF](https://cryptoetf.today) **`C`** - Reports daily net flows for US spot crypto ETFs across thirteen assets, with up to 30 days of history per asset, weekly flow analytics, a 0 to 100 CEFI sentiment index and spot prices. *Use case: Checking the latest net flows into Bitcoin and Ether ETFs, reviewing a week of Solana ETF flows, comparing the sentiment index with spot price moves.*
- [CryptoKeyhole](https://cryptokeyhole.com) **`C`** - Returns read-only on-chain metrics computed from Bitcoin, Ethereum, Litecoin, Dogecoin and Bitcoin Cash nodes plus market data for a growing list of coins and tokens, with each metric's definition, method, source, coverage and observation finality, and reports missing observations as gaps. *Use case: Counting bitcoin blocks mined each day this week, checking Ethereum's circulating supply today, listing which metrics exist for Solana.*
- [cryptonews.is](https://cryptonews.is) **`C`** - Reads cryptonews.is stories grouped from more than 1,000 sources with summaries in 28 languages, searches stories, and returns market data and minute-by-minute news sentiment with history. *Use case: Finding what is moving Bitcoin in the last hour, checking news sentiment for Ethereum since Monday.*
- [Cryptowisser News](https://www.cryptowisser.com) **`C`** - Returns the latest crypto headlines, coverage of a named coin, exchange, company, person or regulator, the week's trending stories, date-filtered archive keyword searches and full Cryptowisser articles with source links. *Use case: Catching up on what moved crypto markets today, pulling the past two weeks of stablecoin regulation coverage, summarizing a full article about an exchange security incident.*
- [DAMO Market State](https://damolive.com) **`C`** - Returns the current market state (bull momentum, weakening, bear momentum or recovery) of 44 crypto assets across 10 timeframes from 1 minute to 1 month, with state definitions and covered symbols, and no prices, forecasts or trade signals. *Use case: Checking a crypto asset's state on the 4h and daily timeframes, comparing current states across several assets at once.*
- [DEBYKO](https://debyko.com) **`C`** - Reads public market data from 16 crypto perpetual-futures venues exactly as published, including prices, funding rates, open interest, order-book depth and candles, with cross-venue screening and point-in-time history. *Use case: Comparing ETH funding rates on Binance and Hyperliquid, screening for instruments with rising open interest, checking what an order book looked like at a past moment.*
- [DeltaFluency](https://deltafluency.com) **`C`** - Lists available BTC and ETH option expiries, runs deterministic strategy scans against current market snapshots and returns strategy candidates with their legs, liquidity, analysis and ranking metrics, without placing trades. *Use case: Scanning ETH option strategies for next month's expiry, comparing the liquidity of two BTC strategy candidates, inspecting the legs of a top-ranked strategy.*
- [Eagle Virtual USDT & USDC Blacklist Tracker](https://eaglevirtual.com) **`C`** - Checks whether Tether, Circle or another stablecoin issuer has blacklisted, frozen or seized a wallet across 41 blockchains, returning dated records with issuer, chain, transaction and amount, and lists recent freezes, the largest blacklistings and issuer freeze statistics. *Use case: Checking a counterparty's Tron address against the USDT blacklist before settlement, finding the largest stablecoin blacklisting on record, listing the latest USDT freezes on Tron.*
- [Etherscan](https://etherscan.io) - Retrieves read-only onchain data across more than 60 EVM chains, including wallet balances, token transfers, transaction history and receipts, verified contract source and ABIs, event logs, blocks by timestamp and gas-oracle prices. *Use case: Tracing where tokens from a wallet moved on Arbitrum, reading a verified contract's source before interacting with it, checking current gas prices on Base before sending a transaction.*
- [Gemini](https://gemini.com) - Reads live crypto market data, order books, candles, and prediction-market contracts, plus read-only account balances, positions, orders, and trade history. *Use case: Checking order-book depth on a spot pair, reviewing open positions and recent fills, browsing contracts on a prediction-market event.*
- [Glassnode](https://www.glassnode.com) **`C`** - Fetches Glassnode on-chain and market metrics for digital assets, browses assets and the metric catalogue with metadata, and reads Glassnode Insights articles. *Use case: Pulling Bitcoin exchange net flows for the last 90 days, comparing long-term holder supply on Bitcoin and Ethereum.*
- [GoldRush](https://goldrush.dev) **`C`** - Returns onchain data across 100+ blockchains, including wallet token balances, portfolio history, transactions, decoded logs, NFTs, token holders, approvals, prices and gas, plus DeFi metrics such as volume, TVL, fees and active users to compare and rank chains and protocols. *Use case: Auditing a wallet's token approvals on Base, tracing a transaction hash, comparing TVL across two DeFi protocols.*
- [HEY Research Lab](https://heyresearch.xyz) **`C`** - Reports whether Robinhood Chain projects, tickers and contracts are still being built, with shipping timelines, changes, contract creators, token market data and linked evidence. *Use case: Checking what a token project shipped this week, finding who deployed a contract.*
- [INDICIA DESK](https://indiciadesk.com) **`C`** - Returns INDICIA DESK crypto options research for BTC and ETH, including tested trading beliefs, dealer gamma regime, call and put walls, DVOL rank, max pain expiry record and a decision journal of wins and losses. *Use case: Checking whether max pain has pulled BTC price toward it at recent expiries, reading today's ETH gamma regime.*
- [InfStones Blockchain Intelligence](https://infstones.com) **`C`** - Diagnoses failed transactions, traces internal transfers, recommends gas strategy, estimates contract interaction costs, checks contract risk signals, summarizes addresses and blocks, and looks up holdings, history, ENS names and prices across multiple blockchains, read-only. *Use case: Finding why a transaction failed, tracing the token flows inside a transaction, summarizing an address's recent activity.*
- [Keel](https://usekeel.io) - Builds systematic strategies for Hyperliquid crypto perpetual futures from descriptions or library templates, backtests them with fees and slippage, compares runs, and monitors strategies running in Keel without placing orders. *Use case: Backtesting a breakout strategy on BTC perps, comparing two strategy variants side by side, forking a verified strategy from the library as a starting point.*
- [Loris Crypto Markets](https://loris.tools) **`C`** - Compares crypto perpetual-futures funding, open interest, volume and liquidity across exchanges, finds funding dislocations and ranks market anomalies from current and historical Loris data. *Use case: Finding the largest BTC funding gap between two exchanges, ranking coins by open-interest growth today.*
- [Lukka Blockchain Analytics & Compliance MCP](https://github.com/lukkatech/lukka-mcps/blob/main/docs/lukka-aml-mcp/README.md) **`C`** - Generates risk scores and compliance reports for blockchain addresses across 108+ chains, including a C-Score, risk level, sanctions matches, mixer and ransomware exposure, owner attribution and counterparty exposure analysis. *Use case: Running a pre-transaction check on a counterparty's wallet, screening a deposit address for sanctions exposure, pulling a full compliance report for exchange due diligence.*
- [Lukka Digital Asset News MCP](https://github.com/lukkatech/lukka-mcps/blob/main/docs/lukka-news-mcp/README.md) **`C`** - Searches a curated digital asset news corpus by keyword, date, publisher, category, sentiment, audience or quality, returning structured records with quality and relevance scores, and provides daily digests with sentiment breakdowns, themes and events. *Use case: Catching up on stablecoin regulatory news from the past week, filtering high-quality Ethereum coverage for a research note, checking the sentiment breakdown for a volatile trading day.*
- [Lukka Pricing & Market Data MCP](https://lukka.tech) **`C`** - Provides institutional digital asset prices, OHLCV candles, VWAP, market capitalization rankings, order book depth and spot and derivative trades across 100+ venues, including Lukka reference rates. *Use case: Pulling an end-of-day mark for ETH on a reporting date, comparing a token's price across exchanges, checking order book depth for a thinly traded token.*

- [Nansen](https://www.nansen.ai) **`C`** - Read-only onchain analytics for tokens, wallets and Smart Money flows across 30+ chains. *Use case: Seeing what Smart Money wallets bought this week, profiling a wallet before a trade, checking how concentrated a token's holders are.*
- [NAVI Protocol](https://naviprotocol.io) **`C`** - Reads NAVI lending pools, reserve configuration, incentive APY and wallet health factors on the Sui blockchain, and prepares supply, withdraw, borrow and repay transactions. *Use case: Checking a wallet's health factor before borrowing, comparing incentive APY across pools, preparing a repay transaction.*
- [OnChainDiligence](https://onchaindiligence.com) - Inspects proposed autonomous-agent payments against caller-supplied policy before execution, retrieves public signed receipts, and verifies receipt proofs, without holding funds, signing keys or executing payments. *Use case: Checking whether an agent's proposed payment fits a spending policy, pulling the signed receipt for a completed payment, verifying a receipt proof during an audit.*
- [Otto AI Stocks](https://useotto.xyz) **`C`** - Researches tokenized stocks, finding a company's token versions on Robinhood Chain, Solana, BNB Chain and Base by issuer, with DEX prices, 24-hour change, volume and pool liquidity, plus idea-based company filters and short profiles. *Use case: Finding the most liquid tokenized version of a chipmaker's shares on Base, listing companies making AI chips that have tokenized versions, comparing pool liquidity across issuers before trusting a quoted price.*
- [paidfor](https://paidfor.dev) **`C`** - Checks x402 sellers against a public index built from paid calls with USDC on Base, returning a verdict, failure rate with a 95% interval, listed and quoted prices and evidence links, and lists reliable sellers and recent index changes. *Use case: Checking an x402 inference endpoint before an agent pays it, finding zero-failure data sellers, reviewing which sellers changed pay-to addresses recently.*
- [Provyan](https://provyan.com) **`C`** - Returns historical studies of whether 44 common crypto trading signals worked on 90 or more coins, the latest signals and evidence for a coin and signal. *Use case: Checking how Ethereum performed a week after past death crosses.*
- [Pyth](https://pyth.network) **`C`** - Lists Pyth's over 500 price feeds across crypto, equities, FX, metals, commodities and rates, fetches live prices with bid/ask spreads and confidence intervals, and pulls historical price snapshots at a timestamp and OHLC candlestick data. *Use case: Pulling candlestick data for a crypto asset to analyze volatility, comparing prices across asset classes, checking the latest FX price with its confidence interval.*
- [Revolut X](https://www.revolut.com/revolut-x/) - Advanced crypto exchange with low fees. *Use case: Crypto trading, market and limit orders, backtesting, portfolio management for active traders.*
- [SatKeepers](https://satkeepers.com) **`C`** - Runs the SatKeepers Bitcoin self-custody checkup, explains terms and returns recovery plan information. *Use case: Running a self-custody checkup and seeing which steps a family inheritance plan is missing.*
- [SatoshiMacro Market Data](https://satoshimacro.com) **`C`** - Reads live Bitcoin cycle data, including a 0-100 cycle confluence score with an Ethereum variant, on-chain cycle indicators, the Altcoin Season Index, US spot Bitcoin ETF flows, ASX Bitcoin ETF assets and the Crypto Fear and Greed Index. *Use case: Checking whether it is altcoin season right now, totaling this week's net inflows into US Bitcoin ETFs, seeing which zone the cycle model puts Bitcoin in today.*
- [Simple Mining](https://www.simplemining.io/bitcoin-mining-calculator) **`C`** - Calculates Bitcoin mining profitability from live hashprice, with break-even at a given electricity price, hashprice history and drivers, bear-to-bull forecasts through the next halving, ASIC miner comparisons, hosting cost estimates and depreciation tax effects. *Use case: Checking whether a specific ASIC model breaks even at 8 cents per kWh, comparing hosting costs with running a miner at home, estimating the first-year tax write-off on a new rig.*
- [SoChain](https://sochain.com) **`C`** - Returns live and historical Bitcoin, Litecoin, Dogecoin and Zcash data, including balances, transactions, unspent outputs, payment status, blocks, fees, prices, charts, rich lists and address-poisoning checks, read-only. *Use case: Checking whether a Bitcoin payment has confirmed, looking up an address balance on a past date, screening a pasted address for look-alike poisoning.*
- [Stable.com](https://stable.com) **`C`** - Looks up supported networks and assets on the Stable platform, gives indicative swap rates and fees, tracks swaps a user has already started and shows wallet balances and token allowances, without executing trades or moving funds. *Use case: Checking the status of a swap already started, getting an indicative rate and fees before swapping on the Stable platform, reviewing wallet balances and token allowances.*
- [The Big Whale MCP](https://www.thebigwhale.io) **`C`** - Searches crypto and digital-asset editorial analysis, scored news, European regulation records, company and people profiles, market indicators, and indexed bank annual reports. *Use case: Checking which MiCA licences a firm holds, tracking stablecoin metrics over a quarter, pulling the digital-asset section of a bank's annual report.*
- [The Coin Daily](https://thecoindaily.co) **`C`** - Returns crypto market research from The Coin Daily, including price charts, funding, open interest, liquidations, order-book depth, sector cohort comparisons, source-linked news and published market updates. *Use case: Charting Bitcoin's move over the past week, comparing DeFi tokens against AI tokens, reading the latest market update.*
- [The Tie Fundraising and M&A](https://www.thetie.io) **`C`** - The Tie provides institutional-grade data on crypto fundraising rounds and mergers and acquisitions, queryable through natural language. *Use case: Look up recent funding rounds and M&A deals in a specific crypto sector to benchmark deal valuations.*
- [Token Terminal](https://tokenterminal.com) **`C`** - Queries standardized on-chain financial and usage metrics across 100+ blockchains, 1,200+ applications and 3,000+ tokenized assets from Token Terminal in natural language, with a Token Terminal account required. *Use case: Checking a protocol's financial metrics, comparing token metrics across two chains, pulling on-chain metrics before a research note.*

- [UNIALL RWA Index](https://uniall.co) **`C`** - Searches tokenized real-world assets across 30+ chains by name, issuer, category or chain, returns sourced fact sheets, checks on-chain transfer restrictions and reports index statistics. *Use case: Comparing tokenized treasury funds by AUM, checking whether a fund token can move to a non-whitelisted wallet.*
- [Unlock Pulse](https://billibo6.github.io/unlockpulse/ai.html) **`C`** - Lists scheduled releases of vested crypto tokens over the next 1 to 60 days, rated strong, medium or small by size against circulating supply and filterable by KuCoin or Binance perpetual futures, with per-token schedules, recipients and USD values and a 2023 to 2026 record of moves against Bitcoin, read-only. *Use case: Finding large token releases due in the next two weeks, checking who receives one token's next release, reviewing how tokens moved against Bitcoin before past releases.*
- [XCPinata — Fine Art](https://xcpinata.com) **`C`** - Searches XCPinata Bitcoin-native artworks and collections, reads artist provenance and current prices and returns links to acquire originals. *Use case: Finding pieces by one artist across XCPinata collections, checking a piece's price and provenance.*
- [노크워치 KnockWatch](https://knockwatch.co.kr) **`C`** - Checks a coin on the Upbit Korean won market against KnockWatch public risk signal records and exchange alerts, lists recent signals and explains signal types, in Korean. *Use case: Checking whether a coin was flagged as a caution item this week, reviewing recent sell-wall signals.*

## Payments Billing and Invoicing

- [Agent Wallet Index](https://agentwalletindex.fyi) **`C`** - Searches a catalog of agent wallets, payment protocols, card rails, payments platforms and key-management layers restated from vendor docs and specs, returning listings with source references for comparison by rail, chain, custody or protocol, plus categories and catalog statistics. *Use case: Comparing agent wallets by custody model and supported chain, finding which payment protocols run on card rails for an agent checkout, pulling the source documentation behind a listing.*
- [AgentCollect](https://www.agentcollect.com) **`C`** - Answers natural-language questions about an accounts-receivable recovery portfolio, covering outstanding invoices, collection status, disputes and recovery performance, read-only. *Use case: Checking which overdue invoices are in dispute, comparing recovery performance this quarter against last, listing accounts still awaiting collection.*
- [Airwallex](https://www.airwallex.com) - Checks Airwallex account balances, FX rates, conversions and transactions, manages billing customers, products, prices, subscriptions and invoices, creates cards, cardholders, beneficiaries and payment links, reviews spend bills and vendors, and searches Airwallex docs. *Use case: Checking cash position across currencies, setting up an invoice from a PO, adding a supplier as a beneficiary from an invoice.*
- [Atalay](https://atalay.tech) **`C`** - Reads Atalay business data across websites, domains with expiry dates, mailboxes, servers, shops, orders and B2B dealers, drafts e-invoices sent to GİB only after confirmation, and manages software project requests. *Use case: Drafting an e-invoice for a shop order, checking which domains expire next month, asking for a fix on a delivered project change request.*
- [Aurato](https://aurato.io) **`C`** - Searches Aurato payment reference data on payment methods, markets, PSPs, banks, card products, schemes and regulators, with relationships, measurements, news and events, and looks up BIC, IIN and MCC codes. *Use case: Finding the main payment methods in India, looking up which bank issues a card IIN, checking what MCC 5812 covers.*
- [B2Brouter](https://www.b2brouter.net) **`C`** - Searches e-invoices by status, number, or period, explains validation errors using official documentation, and tracks tax report status with authorities, read-only. *Use case: Finding out why an invoice was rejected, listing unpaid invoices from last quarter, checking a Verifactu report status.*
- [Beast Insights](https://beastinsights.com) **`C`** - Explains card decline codes, chargeback reason codes and 3-D Secure results, and defines payments terms, giving the likely cause, next steps, the evidence that usually wins a chargeback and who carries 3-D Secure liability, with source links for each answer. *Use case: Working out why a customer's card returned code 05, preparing evidence for a Visa 10.4 chargeback, checking who carries liability after a 3-D Secure attempt.*
- [Belegbote](https://belegbote.de) **`C`** - Lists and reads received e-invoices and credit notes by unread status, date, sender or document number, sends validated e-invoice XML over the Peppol network, and checks whether a company is reachable on Peppol or listed in its public directory. *Use case: Finding unread supplier invoices from last month, sending an invoice XML to a customer identified by VAT number, confirming a new client can receive invoices over Peppol.*
- [BidBook](https://getbidbook.com) **`C`** - Finds BidBook clients and estimates and invoices, reads a document's lines and totals, adds clients, and creates draft estimates and turns estimates into draft invoices. *Use case: Turning a bathroom remodel job description into a draft estimate, listing overdue invoices for March.*
- [BILL](https://www.bill.com) - Reads company payables, receivables and spend data in BILL, including bills, payments, recurring bill templates, invoices, budgets and card transactions, read-only. *Use case: Listing bills due this week, finding a payment by its confirmation number, summarizing card spend against a team budget.*
- [BillDog](https://billdog.io) **`C`** - Sets up BillDog in-app purchases, subscriptions, offerings, paywalls, placements, feature flags, A/B tests, campaigns and journeys for mobile and web apps, and reads experiment and analytics results. *Use case: Creating a monthly subscription paywall for an iOS app, running an A/B test on its price, reading conversion results.*
- [Biller Genie](https://billergenie.com) **`C`** - Reads invoices, recent payments, customer balances, accounts-receivable summaries and aging reports, and searches customers and items in a billing account. *Use case: Listing customers more than 60 days past due, pulling last week's payments, checking the open balance on one account.*
- [BillingMart](https://billingmart.com) - Creates draft invoices, estimates, bills and recurring schedules, manages clients and vendors, records payments, time, expenses and mileage, sends invoices, reminders and statements subject to approval, and runs receivables, payables, cash forecast and tax reports in a BillingMart workspace. *Use case: Converting an accepted estimate into an invoice, logging billable hours for a client, reviewing the accounts receivable ageing report.*
- [Billino](https://billino.io) **`C`** - Creates, issues, marks paid, voids and deletes invoices, looks up and lists invoices and fetches their PDFs, creates and updates recipients and suppliers, browses catalog items and prices, and reads and updates the business profile in Billino, invoicing software for freelancers and small businesses with compliance handling for EU countries and the US. *Use case: Creating and issuing an invoice for a consulting project, marking last month's invoice as paid, downloading an issued invoice PDF.*
- [Billit](https://www.billit.eu) **`C`** - Access your Billit invoices, customers, and suppliers, and check if companies can receive e-invoices via Peppol. *Use case: Pulling up a customer's outstanding invoice, checking a new supplier's details, verifying whether a vendor can accept e-invoices via Peppol before sending one.*
- [billr](https://billr.ch) **`C`** - Tracks time and creates, sends and updates Swiss QR-bill invoices and quotes, sends reminders, marks invoices paid, creates payment links, manages customers, projects, products, suppliers and expenses, and reports open amounts, aging and monthly summaries, asking before anything that emails customers or changes existing data. *Use case: Logging 2.5 hours on a project for yesterday, invoicing all unbilled time for a client, checking overdue invoices in the aging report.*
- [Billstride](https://billstride.com) **`C`** - Creates, updates, finalizes and emails invoice PDFs, copies previous invoices, manages customers and sender settings, sends reminders, exports to DATEV and reports revenue and client insights. *Use case: Billing a repeat client by copying last month's invoice and changing the hours, sending a payment reminder for an overdue invoice.*
- [Billtrust](https://billtrust.com) **`C`** - Answers questions about Billtrust accounts receivable data, including open balances, total receivables, invoice aging, payment timing, late payments, unapplied cash and application backlog. *Use case: Checking which customers have invoices more than 60 days past due, finding how much cash is unapplied this week.*

- [Račun123](https://www.racun123.si) **`C`** - Searches Račun123 invoices, quotes, expenses, customers and items, and returns business summaries and customer history for Slovenian businesses, read-only. *Use case: Listing unpaid invoices over 30 days and comparing this quarter's revenue with last year.*
- [Rent a Trade](https://www.rentatradefl.com) **`C`** - Returns Rent a Trade workspace totals, upcoming appointments and workspace links, and, with separately approved invoice permissions, finds, reads and updates editable unpaid invoices. *Use case: Updating the scope of work and due date on an unpaid drywall invoice before sending it again.*
- [Retail Payment Methods Transparency Index](https://www.ecomstream.eu/payment-methods-transparency-index) **`C`** - Shows which payment service providers offer each payment method to merchants in the EU, Norway, the United Kingdom and Switzerland (excluding Cyprus and Malta) by country and channel, with published prices, pricing models and read dates, and profiles how methods work, including chargeback exposure, refusals, failure reasons and owner fees. *Use case: Finding which providers offer a given payment method online in Germany, listing every price one provider publishes, checking chargeback exposure for a payment method.*
- [Scope to Invoice PDF](https://tools.runstratus.com/invoice) **`C`** - Turns pasted estimates, scopes of work, line items or a photo of a handwritten scope into a line-item invoice PDF with tax, deposit and balance due, plus a short cover email. *Use case: Converting a handwritten deck-repair scope into an invoice with a 30 percent deposit, billing a client for a website build from an emailed estimate.*
- [Sequence](https://www.sequencehq.com) - Reads Sequence customers and aliases, invoices including outstanding ones, billing schedules, products, price lists, credit notes, revenue journals, revenue waterfalls and invoiced revenue. *Use case: Finding overdue invoices to follow up on, reporting on invoiced revenue, checking how a customer is billed.*
- [SIMcloud Reporting](https://simcloud.co.za) **`C`** - Reads SIMcloud wallet balance and transaction history and looks up airtime, data, voucher, electricity and SMS orders by reference. *Use case: Reconciling last month's airtime purchases for a fleet of company phones, checking whether a bulk SMS was delivered.*
- [SimFa](https://simfa.sk) **`C`** - Creates, edits and issues invoices, quotes and credit notes for Slovak businesses, sends them by email or Peppol, sends payment reminders, records payments and reports receivables, costs, cash flow and tax estimates. *Use case: Issuing an invoice to a customer added by company ID, sending a reminder for an overdue invoice.*
- [Skol: Split the Bill](https://getskol.com) **`C`** - Creates, reads, lists and updates itemized Skol bill splits with shared items, tax and tip, and returns a link for friends to pay their share. *Use case: Splitting a dinner receipt for six with two shared appetizers and an 18 percent tip, updating a split after someone leaves early.*
- [SolidInvoice](https://solidinvoice.co) **`C`** - Creates, sends and tracks SolidInvoice invoices, quotes and recurring invoices, manages clients, contacts and tax rates, converts quotes to invoices, records payments and reports revenue and overdue totals. *Use case: Converting an accepted website quote into an invoice, listing overdue invoices over 30 days, setting up a monthly retainer invoice.*
- [Soumow](https://soumow.com) **`C`** - Reads Soumow freelance clients, projects, proposals, invoices, appointments and finance summaries on a PRO or Business plan, creates clients, draft proposals and invoices, adds appointments, expenses and tasks, and when enabled sends invoice reminders after showing them and getting confirmation, at most once per invoice per day. *Use case: Listing overdue invoices and the total outstanding, drafting a proposal for a logo project, booking a client call tomorrow at 3 pm.*
- [SOWND](https://www.sownd.studio) **`C`** - Reads a DJ's own gigs, invoices and earnings, listing unpaid invoices, past gigs not yet invoiced and earnings summaries kept in their original currencies, read-only. *Use case: Counting gigs played this year, listing invoices still unpaid, totaling Q3 earnings.*
- [Spirit Pay](https://spiritpay.fr) **`C`** - Creates and emails Spirit Pay bank transfer payment links, sends reminders, tracks unpaid payments, forecasts collections, analyzes customer payment behavior, updates customers and prepares supplier payment batches for owner approval. *Use case: Sending payment links for 12 March invoices, reminding a client 30 days late, preparing a supplier batch for approval.*
- [Square](https://squareup.com) - Calls the Square API to view transactions, manage customer profiles, track inventory, process payments, analyze sales patterns and generate financial reports. *Use case: Reviewing recent transactions, updating a customer profile, checking inventory levels.*
- [StreamPay](https://streampay.pl) **`C`** - Reads StreamPay creator donation history, statistics and top supporters, reads widget and payment-page settings, and updates donation alert, ranking, goal and countdown widgets and payment-page settings. *Use case: Reviewing last week's top supporters, raising the target on a stream's donation goal widget.*
- [striketip MCP](https://striketip.com) **`C`** - Returns tip performance for team members and managers on a striketip or strikepay account. *Use case: Checking this week's tip performance before a team check-in.*
- [Stripe](https://stripe.com) - Reads and writes Stripe API objects, summarizes balances, runs Stripe analytics, lists and manages accounts, searches Stripe documentation and support articles, and plans Stripe implementations. *Use case: Checking an account balance summary, reading an object through the Stripe API, searching the documentation while planning an implementation.*
- [Sudrel](https://sudrel.com.br) **`C`** - Monitors Brazilian electronic invoices (NFS-e, NF-e and CT-e) received and issued by companies, validates CPF and CNPJ numbers, and prepares and issues NFS-e service invoice batches from spreadsheets after confirmation. *Use case: Checking which supplier invoices arrived this month, issuing a batch of service invoices from an Excel sheet.*
- [Tabs](https://tabs.com) - Queries billing, revenue and contract data in Tabs, reading contracts with their performance obligations and usage, invoices, credit memos, payments, customers, products, renewals, and ARR and cash forecasting reports. *Use case: Listing upcoming renewals, matching payments against a customer's invoices, pulling the ARR and cash forecasting reports.*
- [Tellen](https://tellen.app) **`C`** - Lists, creates, edits, deletes and voids invoices in a Tellen electronic invoicing account for Ecuador, with edits limited to drafts and voids to authorized invoices within the legal deadline, looks up customers, products and emission points, and submits invoices to the SRI for authorization, asking before creating, submitting or voiding a document. *Use case: Drafting an invoice for a consulting client with IVA calculated, submitting it to the SRI, voiding an authorized invoice issued in error.*
- [Tesorio](https://www.tesorio.com) **`C`** - Looks up invoices with status, balance and aging, tracks supplier portal submissions, reviews dunning campaign performance, collections threads and tasks, incoming payments, credit memos and drafted cash matches, and adds notes or updates fields. *Use case: Listing customers 30 or more days past due with their last email, checking how this month's dunning campaigns performed, finding payments the cash application agent could not match.*
- [Tezga eKasa](https://tezgafiskal.com) **`C`** - Issues and fiscalizes Tezga eKasa receipts in Serbia, handles refunds and cancellations, opens and closes advance invoices, sends e-invoices to SEF and reads document status and environment, with receipt-writing on a production account requiring explicit confirmation. *Use case: Issuing a fiscal receipt for a 12,000 dinar sale, refunding one item from yesterday's receipt.*
- [The AR Agent](https://thearagent.com) **`C`** - Lists overdue invoices from QuickBooks or Xero in The AR Agent, reads collection cases and payment promises and drafts customer follow-ups. *Use case: Seeing which customers are 60 days late, drafting a firm reminder for one.*
- [Ticket WiFi Zone](https://ticketwifizone.com) **`C`** - Manages WiFi hotspot ticket sales sold by mobile money in West and Central Africa, creating and updating zones and plans, importing MikroTik tickets, deleting unsold ones, setting ticket status and reading stock, sales, statistics, balances and withdrawals. *Use case: Importing a batch of router tickets, checking which zone sold most this week, raising a plan's price.*
- [TributaSoft](https://tributasoft.ec) **`C`** - Searches and reads TributaSoft's step-by-step guides and FAQs for Ecuadorian electronic invoicing in its TBC system, covering invoices, credit and debit notes, withholdings and the ATS report. *Use case: Finding how to void an invoice already sent to the SRI, asking how to set up a new emission point.*
- [trilha Invoice](https://invoice.trilhahub.cloud) **`C`** - Issues, validates and tracks Brazilian national NFS-e service invoices, checks municipalities, searches fiscal documents and downloads PDF or XML, manages certificates, numbering, fiscal settings, service catalogs and notifications, and checks cancellation and approval status. *Use case: Issuing an NFS-e for a monthly consulting service from a one-line request, checking whether a municipality supports the national NFS-e, downloading the XML of last week's invoices for the accountant.*
- [tugesto](https://tugesto.com) **`C`** - Searches tugesto billing records, creates, sends and manages invoices and quotes, converts quotes to invoices, records payments and manages clients, products and series across companies, running every write only after explicit confirmation in the conversation. *Use case: Turning an accepted quote into an invoice and sending it, recording a payment on last month's invoice.*
- [Upflow MCP](https://upflow.io) **`C`** - Connect Claude to your Upflow AR data — track invoices, monitor collections, and manage cash application in natural language. *Use case: Checking which invoices are past due before a collections call, matching an incoming payment to the right invoice, confirming a customer's outstanding balance.*
- [Upwell](https://upwell.com) **`C`** - Searches and reads freight billing records, including customer invoices and exceptions, payment status, receivables aging, remittances, bank transactions, customer statements, carrier invoices, bills, shipments and stops, and attached proofs of delivery, bills of lading and rate confirmations, with read-only access. *Use case: Checking why a shipper's invoice is stuck in exceptions, summarizing receivables aging for a top customer, pulling the proof of delivery for a disputed load.*
- [Yuno](https://y.uno) **`C`** - Creates payments, customers, subscriptions and payment links while building a Yuno integration, captures, cancels and refunds payments, answers plain-language questions on transactions, approval and conversion rates and provider performance, and searches the Yuno API docs. *Use case: Comparing approval rates across providers, creating a test payment while building an integration, looking up an endpoint in the Yuno API docs.*
- [Zoho Billing](https://www.zoho.com/billing) - Reads and manages Zoho Billing billing and subscription data, such as customers, plans, subscriptions and invoices. *Use case: Listing subscriptions that renew next week, checking unpaid invoices for an annual plan customer.*
- [Zoho Invoice](https://www.zoho.com/invoice) - Manages Zoho Invoice invoices, payments and billing data for small businesses. *Use case: Creating an invoice for 12 hours of consulting, checking which invoices are overdue by 30 days.*
- [好辦 Haoban](https://haoban.app) **`C`** - Finds quotes, invoices, customers, projects and payments for small businesses in Taiwan, shows overdue invoices and outstanding amounts, summarizes revenue and receivables, creates customers and to-dos, and creates or edits quote and invoice drafts with review links. *Use case: Listing overdue invoices and the total still outstanding, turning a client's emailed request into a quote draft.*

## Personal Finance and Consumer Tax

- [2Fin Tax](https://ai2fin.com) **`C`** - Looks up source-cited GST and VAT rates for 193 countries and income, company and capital gains tax for many more, computes GST or VAT on amounts, compares countries and estimates income tax, company tax, capital gains, super and student loan repayments. *Use case: Adding 10 percent GST to a 1,250-dollar Australian invoice, estimating take-home pay on 50,000 euros in Tampere, comparing company tax in the UK and Canada.*
- [AdvisorWorld Reference Data](https://advisorworld.com) **`C`** - Looks up and compares U.S. state income, estate and inheritance tax reference data with field-level citations, and estimates state income tax from substantiated inputs. *Use case: Comparing estate tax exposure across two states before a move, checking an inheritance tax rate against its citation, estimating income tax for a relocation scenario.*
- [Agregator.ro](https://agregator.ro) **`C`** - Returns Romanian data in Romanian, including the day's top news stories with outlet links, bank term deposit rates with net interest, ranked loan offers with APR and reference indices, and National Bank of Romania exchange rates. *Use case: Comparing 12-month deposit rates across Romanian banks, ranking mortgage offers by total cost, checking today's euro reference rate.*
- [Ajuste de alquiler: calculadora IPC e ICL (Argentina)](https://alquiler.aindastudio.com) **`C`** - Calculates Argentine rent increases under the IPC or ICL index from official INDEC and BCRA data, given rent, start date and adjustment period, and lists this month's increases. *Use case: Working out a rent of 400,000 pesos from March under IPC every four months, checking which contracts adjust this month.*
- [api.dinzo.com.br](https://dinzo.com.br) **`C`** - Reads Dinzo personal finance data aggregated through Brazil's Open Finance, including balances and projected balances, credit card bills, spending by category, goals and investments, and creates, edits and deletes transactions, categories, tags and goals after confirmation. *Use case: Totaling supermarket spending for September, listing open credit card bills with their due dates, projecting the account balance at the end of the month.*
- [Appeal Draft](https://appeal-draft.vercel.app) **`C`** - Compares a Minneapolis–Saint Paul home's county assessed value with recorded sales of similar homes, estimates the gap and yearly tax saving, and gives assessment ratios by area and appeal dates. *Use case: Checking whether a house in Saint Paul is assessed 40,000 dollars too high, finding the Ramsey County appeal deadline.*
- [Australian Tax MCP](https://ato-mcp.com.au) **`C`** - Searches over 34,500 Australian tax law documents, including guidance, legislation and public rulings, reads documents and definitions, returns thresholds and runs deduction, depreciation, BAS preparation and audit risk checklists. *Use case: Checking whether a home office deduction applies to a hybrid worker, finding the 2026 instant asset write-off threshold, preparing a BAS checklist.*
- [Bajaj Finance](https://www.bajajfinserv.in) - Reads a customer's Bajaj Finance loans, upcoming EMIs, dues, Flexi limits and EMI Network Card details after mobile number and OTP sign-in, and answers product questions, read-only. *Use case: Checking when the next personal loan EMI is due, seeing how much limit is left on the EMI Card.*
- [Baku](https://usebaku.com) **`C`** - Lists remaining monthly and annual credit card credits, and marks credits as used or reverses that. *Use case: Checking which dining credits are left this month, marking an airline credit as used.*
- [BananaSplit](https://bananasplit.net) **`C`** - Reads BananaSplit balances, groups, friends and expenses, adds and edits expenses and recurring expenses, records settle-up payments and restores deleted items. *Use case: Adding a dinner paid for four housemates, checking who still owes for the ski cabin, recording that a friend settled up.*
- [BankBridge](https://bankbridge.money) **`C`** - Fetches live, read-only data from connected US bank, credit card and brokerage accounts, including balances, transactions, spending summaries, recurring charges, monthly cashflow and investment holdings with gain or loss. *Use case: Totaling restaurant spending by month this year, spotting forgotten recurring subscriptions, comparing income against expenses for the last quarter.*
- [BankJar](https://bankjar.fabriquefutur.com) **`C`** - Ranks Spanish bank offers by net value in euros after tax and fees for a chosen goal such as payroll bonus, savings, account or deposit, compares offers, and ranks bank apps, with a source link and check date per offer. *Use case: Finding the best payroll bonus for a 1,500 euro salary, comparing two savings accounts for 20,000 euros, ranking apps with Bizum.*
- [BankSync](https://banksync.io) **`C`** - Connect your bank, brokerage, and credit accounts to query balances, transactions, holdings, and trades and build live financial dashboards in chat. *Use case: Checking how much was spent on dining out last month, comparing brokerage holdings against a target allocation, building a live dashboard of net worth across accounts.*
- [Bazous](https://bazous.com) **`C`** - Answers household cash-flow questions from balances, income and bills recorded in a Bazous account, forecasting the balance until next payday, listing upcoming obligations, simulating moved payments, marking bills paid and saving profile facts on request. *Use case: Checking how low the balance will go before payday, simulating moving a rent payment by a week, marking the health insurance bill as paid.*
- [Before the Lot](https://beforethelot.com) **`C`** - Calculates car purchase figures for U.S. states, including out-the-door price, dealer quote checks, lease checks, loan payments, rebate versus APR, trade-in position and refinancing, and drafts dealer emails. *Use case: Checking a dealer's out-the-door quote on a used SUV in Ohio, comparing a 0.9 percent APR offer against a cash rebate.*
- [Belvo MCP](https://belvo.com) - Reads personal financial data linked through Belvo, including bank accounts, transactions, bills and investments, INSS records in Brazil and SAT tax and IMSS employment records in Mexico, and generates connection links without moving money. *Use case: Summarizing last month's spending by category, reviewing SAT invoices and tax compliance status, checking employment history in IMSS records.*
- [Blipen](https://www.blipen.com) **`C`** - Reads authorized personal, family and business financial records, including accounts, transactions, card statements, budgets, recurring items, investments, tax analyses and company reports, compares periods, and prepares manual accounts, cards and transactions that are saved only after confirmation in the app, without executing payments, transfers or trades. *Use case: Comparing household spending this quarter against last, reviewing a credit card statement before its due date, drafting a manual cash transaction for confirmation.*
- [bluerate](https://www.bluerate.ai) **`C`** - Searches licensed U.S. mortgage loan officers by state, city, ZIP code, loan type and language, with NMLS IDs, licensed states and reviews, and requests a callback. *Use case: Finding a Spanish-speaking FHA loan officer in San Antonio, requesting a callback about a VA loan.*
- [BoB Currency Rates](https://bob.tools/mcp-currency/) **`C`** - Converts currencies and returns live and historical exchange rates for 140 currencies, with previous-day change and data coverage, without an account. *Use case: Converting 100 Thai baht to dollars, checking the euro to yen rate over the last 30 days.*
- [Boldin](https://www.boldin.com) **`C`** - Reads retirement plan details and forecasts, runs what-if scenarios, spending guardrails and Roth conversion analyses, and applies, reviews or undoes plan changes in a retirement planning account. *Use case: Testing retirement two years earlier, comparing Roth conversion amounts, checking spending guardrails.*
- [Borderless Budget](https://borderlessbudget.com) **`C`** - Reads a Borderless Budget account across currencies in a home currency, including budgets, spending by category and trend, cash flow, recurring bills, holdings and net worth, read-only. *Use case: Checking grocery spending this month across euro and dollar accounts, seeing net worth change over three months.*
- [Budgeez](https://budgeez.com) **`C`** - Manages envelope-method budgets, showing money to assign and envelope balances, recording, correcting and deleting expenses and income, creating, renaming, reordering and deleting envelopes, moving money between envelopes, allocating income from a proposed plan, analysing spending and importing transactions from a connected bank. *Use case: Logging a grocery expense against the food envelope, splitting a paycheck across envelopes from the funding plan, importing last week's bank transactions.*
- [Bywell](https://bywell.org) **`C`** - Reads holdings and allocation from linked brokerages, portfolio summaries, goals and contribution room in a Bywell account, and manages watchlists, earnings reminders, price alerts, investment notes and lessons. *Use case: Checking how concentrated a portfolio is in its top three stocks, setting a price alert on a Canadian bank at 80 dollars, adding an earnings reminder for a held tech stock.*
- [CabuTally](https://cabutally.com) **`C`** - Splits group bills with the fewest payments, creates shared events for trips and dinners, adds expenses, people and to-dos, records repayments and kitty contributions, drafts payment reminders and summarizes who owes whom and where the money went. *Use case: Splitting a dinner bill four ways, logging petrol costs on a weekend trip, drafting a reminder for a friend who still owes money.*
- [Cadence Money](https://www.cadencemoney.com) **`C`** - Reads live Cadence personal finance data, including budgets, categorized transactions, upcoming bills, cash flow, net worth history, investment holdings and goal progress, without changing anything. *Use case: Checking how much is left in the dining budget this week, comparing this month's spending with last month, reviewing progress on a credit card payoff goal.*
- [Cancel My Sub](https://goodturn-mcp.pages.dev/cancelmysub) **`C`** - Gives official step-by-step cancellation instructions for about 70 popular US subscriptions, identifies which subscription an unfamiliar card charge belongs to, and works out the last safe day to cancel with a calendar reminder. *Use case: Working out what a DD DOORDASH line on a card statement is, getting the steps to cancel a gym membership, setting a reminder before an annual streaming renewal.*
- [Candor Finance](https://candor.money) **`C`** - Reviews connected financial accounts for cash flow, spending against budget, recurring bills and price increases, organizes transactions, updates budgets and goals, and saves notes linked to supporting records in a private workspace. *Use case: Spotting a streaming subscription that quietly raised its price, comparing October grocery spending with the budget, noting a refund to chase from a cancelled flight.*
- [CardPoints](https://www.cardpoints.co.uk) **`C`** - Returns what UK loyalty scheme points are worth, how many points each £1 earns, expiry rules and current bonus offers checked at each brand's site, shows which schemes earn at a named shop, and ranks schemes by return per £1. *Use case: Checking what a Nectar points balance is worth before redeeming, finding which loyalty schemes earn at Argos, comparing Clubcard and Boots returns per pound.*
- [CardStack: Credit Card Tracker](https://cardstack.money) **`C`** - Reads a CardStack credit card wallet to track statement credits and benefits before they expire, suggest a card for each spending category, show points balances, valuations and transfer partners, review annual fee value, spending and transactions, and spot missed rewards across Amex, Chase, Capital One and other cards. *Use case: Checking which Amex credits expire this month, picking a card for a dinner out, judging whether a premium card's annual fee paid off this year.*
- [Cardzen](https://cardzen.ai) **`C`** - Compares rewards across cards in a Cardzen wallet, explains card benefits, and tracks credits, expiring credits and upcoming renewals, changing wallet cards or credit usage only with permission and on explicit request. *Use case: Picking which card to use for a large airfare purchase, checking which dining credits expire this month, reviewing annual fee renewals coming up next quarter.*
- [CasitaTax](https://casitatax.com) **`C`** - Estimates Spanish non-resident income tax (Modelo 210) per owner and tax year on imputed and rental income, lists filing deadlines, and looks up town cadastral data, with the rules and sources applied. *Use case: Estimating Modelo 210 for a UK couple's Alicante flat let for three months in 2025, checking the filing deadline for imputed income.*
- [ClearCash](https://clearcash.app) **`C`** - Reads connected financial accounts in read-only mode, including transactions, spending by category and merchant, cash flow, net worth history, investments, loans and shared household groups. *Use case: Checking dining-out spending last month, tracking net worth change this year, reviewing loan payoff dates.*
- [Code actu](https://meilleurcodeparrainage.com) **`C`** - Searches referral and promo codes for brands in France and French-speaking countries, with welcome bonus, referrer reward, conditions and verification date, compares two to five brands, and lists offers by category and the monthly selection. *Use case: Finding a neobank referral code with the largest welcome bonus, comparing energy-provider referral offers.*
- [Contha](https://contha.com) **`C`** - Reads Contha personal and business books for sole proprietors, reporting budgets, cash flow, net worth, profit and loss, Schedule C and tax estimates, and adds, files and deletes transactions and makes rules, asking before each change. *Use case: Checking what can be spent this month after rent, estimating Q4 quarterly tax, refiling 6 misfiled business charges.*
- [CreditUnions](https://creditunions.fyi) **`C`** - Searches a directory of federally insured US credit unions built from NCUA quarterly call report data, returning charter number and type, field of membership, total assets, member count and branch locations, browsable by city, state and eligibility. *Use case: Finding credit unions near a city that a teacher could join, comparing total assets and member counts of two credit unions.*
- [Daffy MCP](https://www.daffy.org) - Reads a Daffy donor-advised fund's balance, portfolio, contributions and donation history, exports fund statements as CSV, searches 1.5M+ US charities by name, cause or EIN, and cancels donations. *Use case: Exporting last year's giving statement for taxes, looking up a food bank's mission and location by EIN.*
- [Dealo](https://getdealo.in) **`C`** - Finds the discounted gift card to buy for a purchase at an Indian shop, showing where to buy it, the discount, the effective rupee price, the card amounts needed and how to redeem it. *Use case: Working out the cheapest way to pay for a ₹6,000 order at an electronics store.*
- [dendryq](https://dendryq.com) **`C`** - Compares credit cards in Portugal and Spain by annual fee and how to avoid it, interest rate, rewards and bank-published APR, with a standardized APR calculated on €1,500 repaid over 12 months and links to each bank's page. *Use case: Listing every card with an avoidable annual fee in Spain, comparing the standardized APR of two Portuguese bank cards, checking one bank's rewards cards.*
- [DIVID Savings & Investment Planner](https://divid.ai) **`C`** - Builds and saves one-year plans for savings already held by US savers new to investing, simulating them against more than 1,000 market scenarios to show goal likelihood, allocation across savings, CDs, T-bills, bond funds, an S&P 500 index fund and gold, and severe-year losses in dollars, and compares what-if changes side by side. *Use case: Testing how likely a down payment goal next fall is, comparing the plan with 20 percent less spending on extras, saving the plan to reopen later.*
- [Don](https://don.financial) **`C`** - Reads selected Don accounts for balances, income and spending over a date range, transaction details, recorded budgets and recurring bills, without moving money or editing anything. *Use case: Comparing income and spending for the last three months, searching for every transaction at one grocery store, listing recurring bills due before the next paycheck.*
- [DoubleTaxation](https://doubletaxation.app) **`C`** - Checks an individual's tax residence between two countries with DoubleTaxation's rules engine, using treaty tie-breaker questions, and compares employee tax burden across countries. *Use case: Working out tax residence after 200 days in one country with a home in another, comparing the tax burden on the same salary in two countries.*
- [Driggsby](https://driggsby.com) **`C`** - Reads bank and investment accounts connected through Plaid, including cash transactions, holdings, debts and recurring transactions, runs queries on them, tracks custom assets and liabilities and emails summaries, with read-only account access that cannot move money. *Use case: Reviewing recurring transactions, checking outstanding debts across accounts, setting up a scheduled review of spending and holdings.*
- [earmark](https://earmarkiq.app) **`C`** - Reads UK bank data connected to earmarkIQ through Open Banking, including accounts, transactions, spending summaries, subscriptions, payday summaries, investments and net worth. *Use case: Comparing grocery spending in October with September, listing subscriptions that went up this year.*
- [Emma App](https://emma-app.com) **`C`** - Reads Emma data, including connected accounts and balances, spending, budgets, recurring payments, savings, investments, goals and transactions. *Use case: Checking how much went on eating out last month, listing all active subscriptions.*
- [Enfy.nl](https://enfy.nl) **`C`** - Compares Dutch electricity and gas contracts for a household's usage with energy tax, grid costs and VAT, estimates switching savings, shows hourly dynamic prices, supplier tariffs and price history, finds the grid operator by postcode and checks home battery payback. *Use case: Comparing one-year fixed contracts for 2,700 kWh and 1,100 cubic metres of gas, checking tomorrow's cheapest hours to run a dishwasher.*
- [Era Context](https://era.app/context) - Personal finance MCP server connecting bank, card, and investment accounts. *Use case: Asking Claude about real balances, spending patterns, recurring charges; automating money movement; cross-agent memory across MCP-compatible AI clients.*
- [EveryPlan](https://everyplan.com.au) **`C`** - Reads usage and rates from an Australian power bill, prices every household electricity plan in the AER register for that network by yearly cost including GST, shows plan details and the network, and gives a switching link. *Use case: Uploading a Brisbane quarterly bill to find a cheaper plan, comparing the daily supply charges of two plans.*
- [facturillo](https://facturillo.com) **`C`** - Searches Panamanian receipts in facturillo with spending, vendor and income summaries, adds receipts by DGI code, upload or hand, compares Panama online store prices with history and promotions, and manages shopping baskets. *Use case: Checking how much went on groceries in October, pricing a weekly basket across three supermarkets.*
- [Family Office](https://unicornrealestate.co.uk) **`C`** - Reads a household's Family Office plan, including net worth, plan outcome and readiness score, FIRE figures by withdrawal rate, largest holdings and financial statements. *Use case: Checking how many years remain to financial independence at a 3.5 percent withdrawal rate.*
- [Fihub](https://fihub.com.br) **`C`** - Reads personal finances from the Brazilian app Fihub, aggregated through Open Finance, including spending, card bills, subscriptions, budgets, cash-flow forecasts, investments and shared-expense groups, and, with permission, sets categories, tags and titles on transactions. *Use case: Finding why spending rose in October compared with September, tagging all Uber rides from a business trip.*
- [Finary](https://www.finary.com) **`C`** - Reads Finary accounts and holdings across bank, brokerage, retirement, crypto, real estate and loan accounts to answer questions on net worth, allocation, fund fees and spending, and simulates compound interest. *Use case: Checking how wealth splits between stocks and real estate, totaling food spending for last month, projecting when saving 500 euros a month reaches financial independence.*
- [Finch Agent](https://finch-agent.com) **`C`** - Provides read-only access to bank accounts, cards, stock, ETF and crypto holdings, savings goals and manually tracked assets aggregated in the Finch personal finance app for Switzerland and Europe, returning transactions, spending summaries, net worth and a daily market snapshot. *Use case: Charting net worth over the last six months, totaling restaurant spending this month, checking progress toward a savings goal.*

- [OweJar](https://owejar.com) **`C`** - Tracks money owed in OweJar, managing people, books, IOUs, bills, shared expense splits, categories, recurring entries and repayments, with changes prepared for review. *Use case: Splitting a 4,800 rupee dinner among four friends, recording that Ravi repaid 1,200.*
- [OweRight IRS Letter Help](https://www.oweright.ai) **`C`** - Explains IRS letters and notices such as CP2000, CP14 and CP504 by code or description, in English or Spanish, with what each means, the deadline rule and the options. *Use case: Understanding a CP2000 notice that proposes extra tax, finding the deadline to reply to a CP14.*
- [Panda Money](https://panda.altotunchitoo.me) **`C`** - Provides read-only access to Panda Money wallets, transactions, categories, budgets, statistics and bill reminders, with transaction search by date, wallet, category, amount or text. *Use case: Checking how much went to restaurants last month against the budget, listing bills due next week.*
- [Patrician.ch: Where to Live, Tax and Second Passports](https://patrician.ch) **`C`** - Ranks and compares places to live on income tax, exit tax, wealth tax, residence routes and household income kept over ten years, returns income tax by place and place profiles, and profiles passports with years to citizenship and investment routes. *Use case: Ranking places to live on a $2M income with a US passport, comparing Dubai and Monaco for a family, checking how long Portuguese citizenship takes.*
- [Pearl](https://joinpearlai.com) **`C`** - Reads linked bank, card, loan, and investment accounts to report spending, subscriptions, budgets, net worth, and goal progress, tagged business or personal. *Use case: Splitting business expenses from household spending before taxes, finding subscriptions that renewed unnoticed, checking whether a savings target is still on schedule.*
- [PennyFinch](https://www.pennyfinch.com) **`C`** - Reads PennyFinch personal finance data, summarizing transactions, spending, budgets, recurring charges, savings goals, net worth and debt payoff projections, and expressing any amount as hours of working life. *Use case: Checking which subscriptions renew in the next 30 days, seeing when an extra $200 a month would clear card debt, finding how many work hours dining out cost last month.*
- [PensionHunter](https://pensionhunter.ai) **`C`** - Returns sourced pension rules and post-departure deadlines for 41 covered countries, assesses a multi-country work history to show where pension records are likely held, and places an employer in a country from its name. *Use case: Checking which pension records exist after working in the Netherlands and Germany, finding the deadline that applies after leaving Finland.*
- [Perfin](https://perfin.finafa.eu) **`C`** - Reads spending by category and month, transactions, balances, budgets, recurring payments and savings goals from a personal finance app for people in Sweden and the Nordics, and creates or edits savings goals on request. *Use case: Checking how much went to restaurants last month, listing subscriptions due before payday, creating a savings goal for a summer trip.*
- [Perkful](https://www.perkful.app) **`C`** - Finds US bank account bonuses, credit card welcome bonuses, store deals, free trials and loyalty offers open now by kind, state or name, and reads an offer's terms, deadline and eligibility. *Use case: Finding checking account bonuses available in Ohio this month, reading the terms of a store gift card bonus before December 31.*
- [PerksRadar](https://perksradar.co) **`C`** - Searches US credit cards tracked by PerksRadar, lists each card's recurring statement credits with amounts, reset cadence and issuer source, shows credits resetting in the next 60 days, and lists point values and transfer bonuses. *Use case: Checking which Amex Platinum credits reset before the end of the month, finding current transfer bonuses to airline partners.*
- [PicPay](https://picpay.com) - Connects agents to PicPay core banking to check balances in real time, manage savings pots, and run operations subject to mobile approval. *Use case: Checking an account balance before a payment, moving funds into a savings pot, approving a requested banking operation from a phone.*

- [RentBuy.org](https://rentbuy.org) **`C`** - Compares renting and buying a home over 30 years in US dollars, running single scenarios with break-even year and net worth, comparing up to ten variants, sweeping inputs to find where the answer flips, and opening an interactive calculator. *Use case: Finding the monthly rent at which buying a $450,000 house wins, comparing a 15-year and a 30-year mortgage side by side, testing how a buyer-only roof replacement shifts the break-even year.*
