## Private Markets Real Estate and Deal Intelligence

- [4liv](https://4liv.com.br) **`C`** - Searches Brazilian homes for sale or rent on 4liv by city, neighborhood, street, type, price, bedrooms, parking and features, reads listing details and prepares alert links with the search criteria. *Use case: Finding two-bedroom apartments to rent in Pinheiros under 4,000 reais with 24-hour concierge, setting an alert for houses with a pool in Campinas.*
- [Acquirepad Real Estate Intelligence](https://www.acquirepad.com) **`C`** - Searches recent commercial real estate deals, leases, newsletters, companies and locations in Acquirepad, read-only, with entry details, map markers, activity stats and sanitized enrichment details. *Use case: Reviewing recent deals and leases near a location before an acquisition, mapping recent market activity.*
- [AgenticPM - Deal Scout](https://www.agenticprivatemarkets.ai) **`C`** - Builds screened target lists for private-markets deal sourcing from published rankings and directories, finds lookalike companies, enriches companies with ownership, parent firm and financial data, and tracks M&A activity and sector news. *Use case: Sourcing founder-owned HVAC services companies, finding lookalikes of a portfolio company, tracking recent add-on deals in the sector.*
- [AI Aste](https://aiaste.it) **`C`** - Searches Italian judicial property auctions from the Ministry of Justice public sales portal by town, province and budget, ranks homes by estimated margin, reads AI analyses of expert appraisals and follows auctions. *Use case: Finding vacant flats at auction in Milan under 150,000 euros, reading what the appraisal says about a Bologna lot before bidding.*
- [ailiste](https://ailiste.app) **`C`** - Finds new-build buildings in Cyprus by city, district, type, bedrooms, stage, delivery year, sea view or pool, shows the stage seen on site, developer details and asking price per square metre by district. *Use case: Finding two-bedroom apartments in Limassol due in 2027 with a pool, comparing price per square metre in Paphos and Larnaca.*

- [The Condo Book Project](https://www.condobooknyc.com) **`C`** - Searches NYC condo offering plans filed with the New York Attorney General and matched to ACRIS recorded sales, returning common charges, taxes, prices per square foot, unit mix, budgets and sponsor teams, comparisons with comps, recorded first sales and page-cited plan excerpts. *Use case: Checking a Brooklyn building's common charges against similar plans before signing, pulling recorded closing prices against the sponsor's price list, quoting what an offering plan says about amenities with its page number.*
- [Totem](https://totemvc.com) **`C`** - Queries fund data including portfolio companies, deals, funds, LPs, company financials, valuations, cap tables, transactions, interactions, notes and news. *Use case: Pulling the latest cap table for a portfolio company before a board meeting, reviewing interactions with an LP ahead of a fundraise, checking portfolio valuations at quarter end.*
- [TreppMCP](https://www.trepp.com/trepp-mcp-use-documentation) - Returns commercial real estate, CMBS and lending data, resolving properties by address, screening properties and loans by geography, property type, performance, vintage or deal structure, and retrieving financials, leases, loan terms, deals, bonds, comparables and pro formas. *Use case: Pulling the loan terms behind an office property, finding comparables for a multifamily asset, reviewing bond-level detail for a CMBS deal.*
- [U-Rite Next](https://www.u-rite.com) **`C`** - Reads and edits the commercial real estate underwriting model open in U-Rite Next for Excel, including properties, rent rolls, expenses and financing, runs the calculation and explains output lines. *Use case: Adding a new 10-year lease to a retail center model, rerunning it to see the change in IRR.*
- [Ultiwrite](https://dwsoffshorepartners.com) **`C`** - Accepts financial statements, merchant processing records, acquisition materials and other commercial documents as evidence for diligence cases, analyzes financial coherence, entity relationships, document integrity, commercial risk and contradictions, and returns case status, findings and exported reports. *Use case: Submitting a target company's financials for an acquisition review, checking whether merchant statements reconcile with reported revenue, exporting the findings report for an investment committee.*
- [UNCG Inventory](https://uncg.engentfy.com) **`C`** - Searches Urban Network Capital Group's new-construction communities, phases and homes by price, bedrooms, size and availability, shows fees, deposit schedules and co-broke terms with record dates, and sends showing requests and messages to the UNCG sales team. *Use case: Finding furnished two-bedroom units under a set price for a client, requesting a Saturday showing of a unit in the newest phase.*
- [ValoNeuf](https://valoneuf.com) **`C`** - Searches new-build real-estate developments for sale in France by town, departement, developer or address, opens development pages with units, areas, prices and delivery dates, describes local markets from DVF sales and permit data, analyses a location's price, and requests an adviser call back. *Use case: Finding new-build apartments around Nantes, comparing a development's prices with recent DVF sales, requesting a call back about a two-bedroom unit.*
- [VDRom](https://vdrom.com) **`C`** - Builds and updates visual fundraising data rooms with sections and components, shows document reads and room visits, pulls a person's or organization's activity across emails, meetings and visits, lists meetings, notes and follow-ups due, drafts follow-up emails, saves email templates and reviews proposed CRM pipeline changes. *Use case: Checking which investors opened the deck this week, drafting a follow-up to a fund that visited the data room twice, adding a financials section to the data room.*
- [Verus by Markst](https://markst.com) **`C`** - Values private-credit loans with a deterministic DCF model, returning low, mid and high prices, sensitivity ladders, scenario stress results, comparable deals and portfolio views, and creates deals from text. *Use case: Pricing a loan from a term sheet, stressing a fund's portfolio under a macro scenario, finding comparables to support a mark.*
- [Viabilix](https://viabilix.com.br) **`C`** - Reads Viabilix real estate feasibility studies, including indicators, income statement, cash flow, scenarios and sensitivity, simulates changes and creates and edits draft studies. *Use case: Simulating a 10 percent rise in construction cost on a 368-unit project, comparing two studies' peak cash exposure.*
- [Vianto Properties - Romania Real Estate Market Data](https://viantoproperties.ro) **`C`** - Returns Romanian real estate market data, including ANCPI transactions and mortgages monthly since 2009 for every county, county-seat city and Bucharest sector, asking prices per square meter for Romania and its largest cities, National Bank of Romania lending, IRCC and ROBOR data, National Institute of Statistics housing and population indicators, the Eurostat house price index and Sibiu market report editions. *Use case: Comparing year-over-year property transactions in Sibiu and Cluj counties, tracking ROBOR against new mortgage lending since 2022, pulling the latest Sibiu market barometer edition.*
- [Visible](https://visible.vc) **`C`** - Queries portfolio company metrics, profiles and notes for investors, and lets founders manage investor contacts and lists, log and tag meeting notes, and read investor update responses. *Use case: Pulling revenue metrics across a fund's portfolio for an LP report, logging notes after a partner meeting, updating a lead investor's contact details.*
- [Yardi Matrix](https://www.yardimatrix.com) - Searches Yardi Matrix properties by location, owner or risk profile, pulls rent trends, occupancy, unit mix, demographics and financial forecasts, benchmarks portfolios against markets, finds distressed assets, maturing loans and acquisition candidates, and generates market intelligence reports. *Use case: Benchmarking a portfolio against its markets, finding distressed assets with maturing loans, comparing rent growth across submarkets.*
- [Ysaere](https://ysaere.com) **`C`** - Traceable AI research swarms for deal teams — due diligence, market, competitive, and angel, intelligence with a receipt. *Use case: Checking whether a target's claims hold up before a term sheet, comparing competitors before a pitch, tracing where a market claim in a research summary actually came from.*
- [ラッコM&A](https://rakkoma.com) **`C`** - Searches and compares company, business and online business sale listings on Rakko M&A, such as websites, social accounts and apps, and returns listing details. *Use case: Finding social media accounts for sale under 1 million yen with stable monthly revenue.*
- [파공넷 — 법원경매·공매·파산공매 (Pagong)](https://www.pagong.net) **`C`** - Searches active Korean court auction, Onbid public sale and bankruptcy trustee sale listings by region, property type and price, adding expected rental yield, market price ratio and assumed tenant-deposit flags to court auction listings, with metric explanations and detail links, read-only. *Use case: Finding apartments at court auction in one district under a price cap, checking whether a buyer would assume a tenant deposit, comparing a listing with nearby market prices.*

## Trading Brokerage and Wealth Management

- [AAWZ](https://aawz.com.br) **`C`** - Reads consolidated client portfolios on the AAWZ platform, including positions, returns, allocation, wealth evolution, transactions, accounts and risk, adds assets and transactions, and supports fixed-fee migration with eligibility, cost comparison, simulations, proposals, presentations and meeting tracking. *Use case: Reviewing a client's allocation by asset class, comparing current costs with a fixed-fee model, generating a migration proposal.*
- [Addepar MCP](https://addepar.com) - Answers questions about a firm's Addepar portfolio data, including household holdings, allocation, performance, alternatives exposure, and transactions, within each user's permissions. *Use case: Preparing for a quarterly client review, checking unfunded commitments across a household, comparing allocation drift between two accounts.*
- [Advisor Copilot](https://www.cognicor.com) **`C`** - Retrieves wealth management client details, signals and priority lists for financial advisors, answers questions about clients and drafts client emails. *Use case: Reviewing which clients to call this week based on new signals, drafting a follow-up email after a portfolio review.*
- [AdvisorFinder](https://advisorfinder.com) **`C`** - Searches public SEC data on US investment adviser representatives and advisory firms by name, firm, city or state, returning registration states, years in the industry, disclosure status, firm locations, Form ADV fee arrangements and disciplinary flags, with links to SEC IAPD and FINRA BrokerCheck. *Use case: Confirming whether a financial advisor is registered in Texas, comparing fee arrangements of two advisory firms, checking a firm for disciplinary flags before signing on.*
- [AIStrategyMiner Strategy Builder](https://aistrategyminer.com) **`C`** - Browses AIStrategyMiner strategy templates and Builder node types, composes and validates MT5 Expert Advisor node flows and returns a link that opens them in the visual Strategy Builder. *Use case: Building an RSI mean-reversion EA for EURUSD with a trailing stop, validating the flow before opening it in the Builder.*
- [Allinvestview](https://www.allinvestview.com) **`C`** - Answers questions about portfolios tracked in AllInvestView, covering holdings, cost basis, performance, dividends, fees, risk and realized gains, and records or corrects trades, books dividends and sets price alerts. *Use case: Checking which holding dragged down performance last quarter, booking a dividend payment the broker sync missed, setting a price alert on a stock ahead of earnings.*
- [Alpaca Live MCP](https://alpaca.markets) **`C`** - Reads a live Alpaca brokerage account's balances, positions, orders, activities and portfolio history, manages watchlists, and pulls stock, option, crypto and index market data, news and corporate actions. *Use case: Summarizing buying power and portfolio concentration, charting equity over the past 30 days, pulling the option chain for a stock before earnings.*
- [Alpaca Paper Trading](https://docs.alpaca.markets/docs/paper-trading) **`C`** - Places, replaces and cancels simulated stock, option and crypto orders in an Alpaca paper trading account, closes positions, exercises options, manages watchlists and pulls market data. *Use case: Buying 10 simulated shares of the best performing holding, closing all paper positions before the weekend, testing an options exercise.*
- [Alrajhi Capital](https://alrajhi-capital.sa/en/) **`C`** - Retrieves Al Rajhi Capital customer holdings across local brokerage, international brokerage, mutual funds and crowdfunding, under the organization's access policies. *Use case: Listing Saudi equity holdings in a local brokerage account, checking mutual fund units held at the end of the quarter.*
- [Amboro MCP](https://amboro.app) **`C`** - Creates, reads, updates, copies and archives applets in an Amboro trading practice account, such as checklists and calculators beside the chart, and deploys them after checking them against Amboro's rules. *Use case: Building a pre-trade checklist applet for the EURUSD chart, copying a position-size calculator to adjust its risk percentage.*
- [Antevo Wealth](https://antevo.ch) **`C`** - Reads a household's wealth records, including net worth, holdings, allocation, exposure, risk, drift, credit facilities, goals, policy breaches and upcoming market events, and creates portfolios and records existing positions after approval. *Use case: Checking household exposure to a single sector, reviewing portfolio drift against policy limits, recording an existing stock position.*
- [Appraisal Bureau](https://appraisalbureau.com) **`C`** - Searches an art collection's artworks, locations, documents, reports and appraisal history, analyzes value changes and acquisition gains, identifies works that may warrant updated valuation, and adds market context from public auction results. *Use case: Listing every work by one artist held at a particular storage location, finding pieces due for reappraisal for insurance, checking recent auction sales by artists in the collection.*
- [Arcana](https://www.arcana.io) **`C`** - Analyzes portfolio and single-stock risk and performance in Arcana, attributing returns across names, factors, and custom groupings. *Use case: Checking how a book has performed year to date, attributing last quarter's returns to factors, reviewing single-stock risk before a rebalance.*
- [B2PRIME](https://b2prime.com) **`C`** - Provides read-only access to a B2Prime trading account, including balances, margin, open and closed positions, order and execution history, plus live market tickers, order books and market details. *Use case: Summarizing open positions and margin usage, reconciling last month's fills against personal trading records.*
- [BaseEdge Labs Account](https://baseedgelabs.ai) **`C`** - Lists BaseEdge Labs strategies and the TradingView presets an account's licenses open, explains preset settings and results, and exports presets to import into TradingView or HyperNull. *Use case: Finding the licensed MNQ preset for a 5-minute chart, exporting it to import into TradingView.*

- [Black Diamond](https://www.sscblackdiamond.com) - Looks up SS&C Black Diamond clients, accounts, holdings, performance, fees, and transactions, and launches rebalancing and tax-loss-harvesting sessions. *Use case: Preparing a performance summary before a client meeting, finding tax-loss harvesting candidates across a book, checking which fees hit an account last quarter.*

- [Plutus | Investment Adviser](https://www.runplutus.com) **`C`** - Reads Plutus strategy allocations and historical strategy factors, stock and option holdings, balances and buying power, daily performance, executed trades and stored orders, searches baskets, portfolios and managed strategies, and retrieves benchmark factor history. *Use case: Checking buying power before a planned options trade, comparing a strategy's factors against a benchmark's history, reviewing which trades executed last week.*
- [POEMS](https://www.poems.com.sg) **`C`** - Reads a POEMS brokerage account's holdings across exchanges and unit trusts, available balance, buying power, margin and margin calls, and realized profit and loss by period, read-only. *Use case: Checking buying power before adding to a Singapore bank stock position, pulling realized profit and loss month by month for this year.*
- [Portfolio Tracker by ValuEdge](https://valuedge.app/connect/portfolio-tracker-mcp) **`C`** - Reads ValuEdge portfolio holdings and allocation, analyzes concentration and risk, and records, edits and removes manual portfolio transactions. *Use case: Checking how much of a portfolio sits in its five largest positions, recording a purchase of 5 AAPL shares at 200 dollars.*
- [porticific](https://porticific.vercel.app) **`C`** - Reads porticific holdings, trades, buy theses and claims, watchlist, cash, DCA plans, prices and analysis, records buys, sells and cash events, runs thesis checks and daily analysis, and saves analysis notes. *Use case: Recording a buy of 10 AAPL at 190 today, checking the claim status of the NVDA thesis.*
- [Production Truthifi MCP](https://truthifi.com) **`C`** - Retrieves holdings, performance, fees, allocation, transactions, and portfolio health findings across linked investment accounts. *Use case: Comparing portfolio performance to a benchmark, totaling fees across accounts, spotting concentrated positions inside funds.*
- [PropPassCheck](https://proppasscheck.com) **`C`** - Compares prop firm challenges by profit target, loss limits, drawdown type and payout rules, tests trading strategies against rules, finds suitable account sizes and tracks rule changes and price history. *Use case: Testing whether a strategy with a 4 percent worst day would pass a 100,000-dollar challenge at two firms.*
- [PU Prime](https://www.puprime.com) **`C`** - Searches PU Prime instruments and returns the latest quotes and instrument details for forex, commodities, indices, shares and ETFs offered as CFDs. *Use case: Checking the latest gold quote and the contract details for the XAUUSD CFD.*
- [Public Holdings](https://public.com) - Reads Public brokerage accounts, portfolio holdings, orders and tax lots, returns quotes, price history, option chains and Greeks, bond and event contract details, and preflights equity, option spread, short, multileg and event contract orders. *Use case: Reviewing gains by tax lot before year end, comparing option Greeks across expirations, preflighting a put credit spread to see its cost.*
- [Puthouse](https://puthouse.com) **`C`** - Reads a Puthouse account's equity, cash, buying power, positions, open and recent orders, closed option trades with realized profit or loss, automated trading status, trade notes, playbooks, stock quotes and option chains. *Use case: Reviewing realized profit on closed puts this month, checking open orders and buying power.*
- [Quantfury](https://quantfury.com) **`C`** - Reads a Quantfury trading account balance, open positions, trade history, limit orders, dividends, rewards, watchlists and instruments, read-only. *Use case: Reviewing realized profit on closed trades this month and listing pending limit orders.*
- [Quantplay](https://www.quantplay.tech) **`C`** - Reads positions, holdings, orders, margins, live P and L and history across more than 10 Indian broker accounts such as Zerodha and Upstox, plus quotes, option chains and historical data. *Use case: Checking total live P and L across Zerodha and Upstox accounts during market hours.*
- [QuantStark](https://quantstark.com) **`C`** - Analyzes QuantStark portfolios for factor exposures, risk, performance attribution and red flags such as hidden overlap, screens stocks, compares ETFs and models trades and holding updates. *Use case: Finding how much of one stock a portfolio really holds through two overlapping ETFs and modelling a trim.*
- [Questrade](https://www.questrade.com) - Reads Questrade account balances, activities and positions, returns market data, and creates watchlists, custom indexes and order or option instructions to discuss trades. *Use case: Reviewing positions in one account and creating a watchlist of Canadian bank stocks.*
- [ScalperIntel AI](https://scalperintel.com) **`C`** - Answers cited questions about ScalperIntel's NinjaTrader 8 indicators, settings, setup and license types, and after email sign-in lists the customer's own licenses, diagnoses license problems, submits a new machine ID, switches activation type and logs support issues, with no trading advice. *Use case: Diagnosing why a ScalperIntel license won't activate, finding out what an indicator setting does, moving a license to a new machine ID.*
- [Schwab Advisor Services](https://sac.schwabinstitutional.com) - Reads Schwab Advisor Center data for advisors who custody with Schwab, including accounts, holders and owners, balances, positions and unrealized gain and loss lots, transactions and realized gains, money movement, standing authorizations, tax withholding elections, alerts, client inquiries, status feeds and preferences, without write access. *Use case: Reviewing a household's positions and unrealized losses before a tax-loss harvesting call, checking open alerts across master accounts, confirming a client's recent money movement.*
- [Slatemark](https://slatemark.ai) **`C`** - Pulls market prices typically about 15 minutes delayed, SEC filings and macroeconomic data, runs technical, portfolio and volatility analyses, and keeps a private trade journal, with read-only brokerage data and option-chain snapshots on Plus, and no order placement or money movement. *Use case: Checking a stock's quote and recent SEC filings, reviewing closed trades in the journal against recorded rules, measuring portfolio concentration risk.*
- [SPARK by SILEX](https://www.silex-partners.com/technology) **`C`** - Searches structured products in a SPARK portfolio, reads term sheets and actively managed product details, tracks lifecycle events such as autocall observations, coupon payments and redemptions, and monitors upcoming maturities, for SPARK clients with an active account. *Use case: Checking upcoming autocall observations and coupon payments, pulling a product's term sheet, listing maturities coming up.*
- [Tradefloor](https://tradefloor.dev) **`C`** - Runs a simulated stock market for testing trading strategies and agents, opening sessions, placing simulated orders, advancing time, forking scenarios and producing report cards and benchmark scores, with no real money. *Use case: Testing a momentum strategy in a simulated session, forking a run to test a rate shock, comparing two strategies against baselines.*
- [Trellia Wealth Partners](https://trelliawealth.com.au) **`C`** - Reads managed-account data from the Trellia Adviser Portal, including portfolio lists, nested portfolios, holdings, performance, fees, updates and SAA records. *Use case: Pulling the holdings and fees of a managed portfolio before a client review, comparing performance across nested portfolios.*
- [Truecast](https://truecast.co.uk) **`C`** - Reads UK financial advisers' client cashflow plans in Truecast, runs retirement timing, Inheritance Tax, contribution, gift and Monte Carlo what-if calculations, solves client goals, saves scenarios and drafts client intake and update changes for firm confirmation. *Use case: Finding a client's earliest affordable retirement date, testing how a one-off gift affects Inheritance Tax.*
- [uSMART SG](https://www.usmart.sg) **`C`** - Reads uSMART Singapore brokerage orders and order details and previews an order's lot size, maximum quantity and buying power, without placing trades. *Use case: Listing last month's filled orders, checking the maximum DBS shares buyable before placing an order.*
- [Vanguard Advisor Tools](https://advisors.vanguard.com) - Returns Vanguard model portfolio data, including expense ratios, asset classes, start dates, and portfolio characteristics, for publicly available US models. *Use case: Comparing expense ratios across two model portfolios, matching a model to a target allocation, checking asset-class mix before an implementation.*
- [Vega](https://vegaminds.com) **`C`** - Searches clients, households, meetings, AI notes, transcripts, and documents from a financial advisor's workspace, read-only. *Use case: Preparing for a client review meeting, finding action items from last quarter's call, reading a client's uploaded document.*
- [Wealth.com](https://www.wealth.com) - Retrieves client estate snapshots, planning risks, tax lookback reports, and document intelligence from wills, trusts, tax returns, and statements for advisory teams. *Use case: Preparing an estate summary before a client meeting, flagging gaps in a trust structure, checking what a prior-year return implies for planning.*
- [Wealthbox](https://www.wealthbox.com) - CRM for financial advisors covering contacts, notes, opportunities, tasks, projects, workflow templates, calendar events, custom objects and fields, tags, and team lookups. *Use case: Prepping for a client meeting from the notes and events on record, logging an opportunity afterward, linking related household contacts together.*
- [WealthSnap](https://wealthsnap.io) **`C`** - Searches Wealthbox contacts, lists WealthSnap form templates and sends client intake and update forms by text or email. *Use case: Sending a beneficiary update form to a client household by text before their annual review.*
- [Webull](https://www.webull.com) - Multi-asset brokerage platform for trading stocks, options, ETFs, and other securities. *Use case: Pull market data and analyze positions across stocks, options, and other assets from a Webull account.*
- [マネックス証券](https://www.monex.co.jp) **`C`** - Reads a Monex Securities account summary, stock, fund, bond and other positions, order history, deposits and withdrawals, and recurring investment settings. *Use case: Checking the total value of Japanese stock and fund holdings, reviewing last month's fund orders and tsumitate settings.*
